#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if (!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

codex
The benchmark currently replicates the full matrix on one process and has no MPI linkage. I’m going to use a 2D block-cyclic tile layout with a blocked right-looking Cholesky: diagonal tiles are factored by their owner, triangular solves form the panel, and panel tiles are exchanged with row/column collectives before distributed trailing updates. This keeps the working factor distributed while preserving the existing CLI and result/validation formats.

+struct DistributedMatrix {
+    size_t n = 0;
+    int tile_size = 0;
+    int tile_count = 0;
+    int process_rows = 0;
+    int process_cols = 0;
+    int my_row = 0;
+    int my_col = 0;
+    size_t local_tile_rows = 0;
+    size_t local_tile_cols = 0;
+    size_t tile_elements = 0;
+    std::vector<double> values;
+
+    DistributedMatrix() = default;
+
+    DistributedMatrix(const size_t matrix_size,
+                      const int block_size,
+                      const int grid_rows,
+                      const int grid_cols,
+                      const int process_row,
+                      const int process_col)
+        : n(matrix_size),
+          tile_size(block_size),
+          process_rows(grid_rows),
+          process_cols(grid_cols),
+          my_row(process_row),
+          my_col(process_col),
+          tile_elements(static_cast<size_t>(block_size) * block_size) {
+        const size_t tile_count_size = (n + tile_size - 1) / tile_size;
+        tile_count = static_cast<int>(tile_count_size);
+        local_tile_rows = tile_count <= my_row
+                              ? 0
+                              : 1 + static_cast<size_t>(tile_count - 1 - my_row) /
+                                    process_rows;
+        local_tile_cols = tile_count <= my_col
+                              ? 0
+                              : 1 + static_cast<size_t>(tile_count - 1 - my_col) /
+                                    process_cols;
+        values.resize(local_tile_rows * local_tile_cols * tile_elements, 0.0);
+    }
+
+    size_t tileRows(const int tile_row) const {
+        const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(tile_row) * tile_size;
+        return std::min(static_cast<size_t>(tile_size), n - first);
+    }
+
+    size_t tileCols(const int tile_col) const {
+        const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(tile_col) * tile_size;
+        return std::min(static_cast<size_t>(tile_size), n - first);
+    }
+
+    double* tile(const int tile_row, const int tile_col) {
+        const size_t local_row =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_row - my_row) / process_rows;
+        const size_t local_col =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_col - my_col) / process_cols;
+        const size_t offset =
+            (local_row * local_tile_cols + local_col) * tile_elements;
+        return values.data() + offset;
+    }
+
+    const double* tile(const int tile_row, const int tile_col) const {
+        const size_t local_row =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_row - my_row) / process_rows;
+        const size_t local_col =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_col - my_col) / process_cols;
+        const size_t offset =
+            (local_row * local_tile_cols + local_col) * tile_elements;
+        return values.data() + offset;
+    }
+};
+
+int mpiCount(const size_t count) {
+    if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI message is too large for the MPI count interface\n");
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+    }
+    return static_cast<int>(count);
+}
+
+void packFromGlobal(const std::vector<double>& global,
+                    DistributedMatrix& local) {
+    std::fill(local.values.begin(), local.values.end(), 0.0);
+
+    for (int tile_row = local.my_row; tile_row < local.tile_count;
+         tile_row += local.process_rows) {
+        const size_t rows = local.tileRows(tile_row);
+        for (int tile_col = local.my_col; tile_col < local.tile_count;
+             tile_col += local.process_cols) {
+            if (tile_row < tile_col) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t cols = local.tileCols(tile_col);
+            double* destination = local.tile(tile_row, tile_col);
+            for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+                const size_t global_row =
+                    static_cast<size_t>(tile_row) * local.tile_size + row;
+                for (size_t col = 0; col < cols; ++col) {
+                    const size_t global_col =
+                        static_cast<size_t>(tile_col) * local.tile_size + col;
+                    destination[row * local.tile_size + col] =
+                        global[global_row * local.n + global_col];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void unpackToGlobal(const DistributedMatrix& local,
+                    std::vector<double>& global) {
+    for (int tile_row = local.my_row; tile_row < local.tile_count;
+         tile_row += local.process_rows) {
+        const size_t rows = local.tileRows(tile_row);
+        for (int tile_col = local.my_col; tile_col < local.tile_count;
+             tile_col += local.process_cols) {
+            if (tile_row < tile_col) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t cols = local.tileCols(tile_col);
+            const double* source = local.tile(tile_row, tile_col);
+            for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+                const size_t global_row =
+                    static_cast<size_t>(tile_row) * local.tile_size + row;
+                for (size_t col = 0; col < cols; ++col) {
+                    const size_t global_col =
+                        static_cast<size_t>(tile_col) * local.tile_size + col;
+                    global[global_row * local.n + global_col] =
+                        source[row * local.tile_size + col];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void distributeInitialMatrix(const std::vector<double>& global,
+                             DistributedMatrix& local,
+                             MPI_Comm cartesian,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int root) {
+    const int count = mpiCount(local.values.size());
+
+    if (rank == root) {
+        for (int destination_rank = 0; destination_rank < local.process_rows * local.process_cols;
+             ++destination_rank) {
+            int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+            MPI_Cart_coords(cartesian, destination_rank, 2, coordinates);
+            DistributedMatrix destination(local.n,
+                                          local.tile_size,
+                                          local.process_rows,
+                                          local.process_cols,
+                                          coordinates[0],
+                                          coordinates[1]);
+            packFromGlobal(global, destination);
+
+            const int destination_count = mpiCount(destination.values.size());
+            if (destination_rank == root) {
+                local.values.swap(destination.values);
+            } else {
+                MPI_Send(destination.values.data(),
+                         destination_count,
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         destination_rank,
+                         100,
+                         cartesian);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        MPI_Recv(local.values.data(),
+                 count,
+                 MPI_DOUBLE,
+                 root,
+                 100,
+                 cartesian,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Barrier(cartesian);
+}
+
+void factorDiagonal(double* diagonal,
+                    const size_t block_size,
+                    const int leading_dimension,
+                    bool& success) {
+    success = true;
+
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < block_size; ++row) {
+        for (size_t col = 0; col <= row; ++col) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < col; ++k) {
+                sum += diagonal[row * leading_dimension + k] *
+                       diagonal[col * leading_dimension + k];
+            }
+
+            if (row == col) {
+                const double value =
+                    diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] - sum;
+                if (value <= 0.0) {
+                    success = false;
+                    return;
+                }
+                diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] = sqrt(value);
+            } else {
+                diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] =
+                    (diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] - sum) /
+                    diagonal[col * leading_dimension + col];
+            }
+        }
+
+        for (size_t col = row + 1; col < static_cast<size_t>(leading_dimension);
+             ++col) {
+            diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] = 0.0;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Solve X * L^T = B for each row of B.  L is lower triangular and both B and
+// L are stored row-major with a fixed tile leading dimension.
+void triangularSolve(double* panel_tile,
+                     const double* diagonal,
+                     const size_t rows,
+                     const size_t columns,
+                     const int leading_dimension) {
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        for (size_t col = 0; col < columns; ++col) {
+            double value = panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + col];
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < col; ++k) {
+                value -= panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + k] *
+                         diagonal[col * leading_dimension + k];
+            }
+            panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + col] =
+                value / diagonal[col * leading_dimension + col];
+        }
+        for (size_t col = columns; col < static_cast<size_t>(leading_dimension);
+             ++col) {
+            panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + col] = 0.0;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// C -= A * B^T.  A and B are transposed panel tiles, so the innermost loop
+// operates on contiguous data in both the update tile and B.  This gives the
+// compiler a simple SIMD-friendly kernel without requiring a BLAS dependency.
+void rankKUpdate(double* C,
+                 const double* A_transposed,
+                 const double* B_transposed,
+                 const size_t rows,
+                 const size_t columns,
+                 const size_t inner,
+                 const int leading_dimension) {
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < inner; ++k) {
+        const double* a = A_transposed + k * leading_dimension;
+        const double* b = B_transposed + k * leading_dimension;
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+            double* c = C + row * leading_dimension;
+            const double scalar = a[row];
+#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
+#pragma GCC ivdep
+#endif
+            for (size_t col = 0; col < columns; ++col) {
+                c[col] -= scalar * b[col];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(DistributedMatrix& matrix,
+                           MPI_Comm cartesian,
+                           MPI_Comm row_communicator,
+                           MPI_Comm column_communicator,
+                           const int rank) {
+    const int tile_count = matrix.tile_count;
+    const int leading_dimension = matrix.tile_size;
+    const size_t tile_elements = matrix.tile_elements;
+    const int root_count = matrix.process_rows * matrix.process_cols;
+
+    std::vector<double> diagonal(tile_elements, 0.0);
+    const size_t max_panel_tiles =
+        tile_count == 0
+            ? 0
+            : (static_cast<size_t>(tile_count) + matrix.process_rows - 1) /
+                  matrix.process_rows;
+    std::vector<double> row_panel(std::max<size_t>(1, max_panel_tiles * tile_elements),
+                                  0.0);
+    std::vector<double> panel(std::max<size_t>(1,
+                                               static_cast<size_t>(tile_count) *
+                                                   tile_elements),
+                              0.0);
+    std::vector<int> panel_counts(matrix.process_rows, 0);
+    std::vector<int> panel_displacements(matrix.process_rows, 0);
+    std::vector<int> panel_offsets(tile_count, 0);
+
+    for (int k = 0; k < tile_count; ++k) {
+        const int diagonal_coordinates[2] = {
+            k % matrix.process_rows, k % matrix.process_cols};
+        int diagonal_owner = 0;
+        MPI_Cart_rank(cartesian, diagonal_coordinates, &diagonal_owner);
+
+        int factor_ok = 1;
+        if (rank == diagonal_owner) {
+            const size_t block_size = matrix.tileRows(k);
+            bool local_ok = true;
+            factorDiagonal(matrix.tile(k, k),
+                           block_size,
+                           leading_dimension,
+                           local_ok);
+            factor_ok = local_ok ? 1 : 0;
+            if (!local_ok) {
+                printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+                       static_cast<size_t>(k) * matrix.tile_size);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Bcast(&factor_ok, 1, MPI_INT, diagonal_owner, cartesian);
+        if (factor_ok == 0) {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        if (rank == diagonal_owner) {
+            std::copy(matrix.tile(k, k),
+                      matrix.tile(k, k) + tile_elements,
+                      diagonal.begin());
+        }
+
+        // Every owner of a tile in block column k needs L(k,k).  Only the
+        // process column containing the diagonal participates in this
+        // broadcast.
+        if (matrix.my_col == k % matrix.process_cols) {
+            MPI_Bcast(diagonal.data(),
+                      mpiCount(tile_elements),
+                      MPI_DOUBLE,
+                      k % matrix.process_rows,
+                      column_communicator);
+
+            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                triangularSolve(matrix.tile(tile_row, k),
+                                diagonal.data(),
+                                matrix.tileRows(tile_row),
+                                matrix.tileCols(k),
+                                leading_dimension);
+            }
+        }
+
+        int total_panel_doubles = 0;
+        for (int process_row = 0; process_row < matrix.process_rows;
+             ++process_row) {
+            panel_displacements[process_row] = total_panel_doubles;
+            int row_tile_count = 0;
+            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows == process_row) {
+                    panel_offsets[tile_row] =
+                        total_panel_doubles + row_tile_count *
+                                                     mpiCount(tile_elements);
+                    ++row_tile_count;
+                }
+            }
+            panel_counts[process_row] = row_tile_count * mpiCount(tile_elements);
+            total_panel_doubles += panel_counts[process_row];
+        }
+
+        std::fill(row_panel.begin(), row_panel.end(), 0.0);
+        if (matrix.my_col == k % matrix.process_cols) {
+            int row_tile_number = 0;
+            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                const size_t rows = matrix.tileRows(tile_row);
+                const size_t columns = matrix.tileCols(k);
+                const double* source = matrix.tile(tile_row, k);
+                double* destination =
+                    row_panel.data() + row_tile_number * tile_elements;
+                for (size_t column = 0; column < columns; ++column) {
+                    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+                        destination[column * leading_dimension + row] =
+                            source[row * leading_dimension + column];
+                    }
+                }
+                ++row_tile_number;
+            }
+        }
+
+        // First make each process row's part of the panel available to every
+        // process column, then allgather those parts down each process column.
+        // After these two collectives every process owns a read-only copy of
+        // the current panel, while the factor itself remains distributed.
+        MPI_Bcast(row_panel.data(),
+                  panel_counts[matrix.my_row],
+                  MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  k % matrix.process_cols,
+                  row_communicator);
+        MPI_Allgatherv(row_panel.data(),
+                       panel_counts[matrix.my_row],
+                       MPI_DOUBLE,
+                       panel.data(),
+                       panel_counts.data(),
+                       panel_displacements.data(),
+                       MPI_DOUBLE,
+                       column_communicator);
+
+        for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+            if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t rows = matrix.tileRows(tile_row);
+            const double* left_panel = panel.data() + panel_offsets[tile_row];
+            for (int tile_col = k + 1; tile_col <= tile_row; ++tile_col) {
+                if (tile_col % matrix.process_cols != matrix.my_col) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                rankKUpdate(matrix.tile(tile_row, tile_col),
+                            left_panel,
+                            panel.data() + panel_offsets[tile_col],
+                            rows,
+                            matrix.tileCols(tile_col),
+                            matrix.tileCols(k),
+                            leading_dimension);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    (void)root_count;
+    return true;
+}
+
+void gatherResult(const DistributedMatrix& local,
+                  std::vector<double>& global,
+                  MPI_Comm cartesian,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int root) {
+    const int process_count = local.process_rows * local.process_cols;
+    const int local_count = mpiCount(local.values.size());
+
+    if (rank == root) {
+        std::fill(global.begin(), global.end(), 0.0);
+        unpackToGlobal(local, global);
+
+        for (int source_rank = 0; source_rank < process_count; ++source_rank) {
+            if (source_rank == root) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+            MPI_Cart_coords(cartesian, source_rank, 2, coordinates);
+            DistributedMatrix source(local.n,
+                                     local.tile_size,
+                                     local.process_rows,
+                                     local.process_cols,
+                                     coordinates[0],
+                                     coordinates[1]);
+            MPI_Recv(source.values.data(),
+                     mpiCount(source.values.size()),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     source_rank,
+                     200,
+                     cartesian,
+                     MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            unpackToGlobal(source, global);
+        }
+    } else {
+        MPI_Send(local.values.data(),
+                 local_count,
+                 MPI_DOUBLE,
+                 root,
+                 200,
+                 cartesian);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Barrier(cartesian);
+}

+bool parseSize(const char* text, size_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(
+                     std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

+struct DistributedMatrix {
+    size_t n = 0;
+    int tile_size = 0;
+    int tile_count = 0;
+    int process_rows = 0;
+    int process_cols = 0;
+    int my_row = 0;
+    int my_col = 0;
+    size_t local_tile_rows = 0;
+    size_t local_tile_cols = 0;
+    size_t tile_elements = 0;
+    std::vector<double> values;
+
+    DistributedMatrix() = default;
+
+    DistributedMatrix(const size_t matrix_size,
+                      const int block_size,
+                      const int grid_rows,
+                      const int grid_cols,
+                      const int process_row,
+                      const int process_col)
+        : n(matrix_size),
+          tile_size(block_size),
+          process_rows(grid_rows),
+          process_cols(grid_cols),
+          my_row(process_row),
+          my_col(process_col),
+          tile_elements(static_cast<size_t>(block_size) * block_size) {
+        const size_t tile_count_size = (n + tile_size - 1) / tile_size;
+        tile_count = static_cast<int>(tile_count_size);
+        local_tile_rows = tile_count <= my_row
+                              ? 0
+                              : 1 + static_cast<size_t>(tile_count - 1 - my_row) /
+                                    process_rows;
+        local_tile_cols = tile_count <= my_col
+                              ? 0
+                              : 1 + static_cast<size_t>(tile_count - 1 - my_col) /
+                                    process_cols;
+        values.resize(local_tile_rows * local_tile_cols * tile_elements, 0.0);
+    }
+
+    size_t tileRows(const int tile_row) const {
+        const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(tile_row) * tile_size;
+        return std::min(static_cast<size_t>(tile_size), n - first);
+    }
+
+    size_t tileCols(const int tile_col) const {
+        const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(tile_col) * tile_size;
+        return std::min(static_cast<size_t>(tile_size), n - first);
+    }
+
+    double* tile(const int tile_row, const int tile_col) {
+        const size_t local_row =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_row - my_row) / process_rows;
+        const size_t local_col =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_col - my_col) / process_cols;
+        const size_t offset =
+            (local_row * local_tile_cols + local_col) * tile_elements;
+        return values.data() + offset;
+    }
+
+    const double* tile(const int tile_row, const int tile_col) const {
+        const size_t local_row =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_row - my_row) / process_rows;
+        const size_t local_col =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_col - my_col) / process_cols;
+        const size_t offset =
+            (local_row * local_tile_cols + local_col) * tile_elements;
+        return values.data() + offset;
+    }
+};
+
+int mpiCount(const size_t count) {
+    if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI message is too large for the MPI count interface\n");
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+    }
+    return static_cast<int>(count);
+}
+
+void packFromGlobal(const std::vector<double>& global,
+                    DistributedMatrix& local) {
+    std::fill(local.values.begin(), local.values.end(), 0.0);
+
+    for (int tile_row = local.my_row; tile_row < local.tile_count;
+         tile_row += local.process_rows) {
+        const size_t rows = local.tileRows(tile_row);
+        for (int tile_col = local.my_col; tile_col < local.tile_count;
+             tile_col += local.process_cols) {
+            if (tile_row < tile_col) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t cols = local.tileCols(tile_col);
+            double* destination = local.tile(tile_row, tile_col);
+            for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+                const size_t global_row =
+                    static_cast<size_t>(tile_row) * local.tile_size + row;
+                for (size_t col = 0; col < cols; ++col) {
+                    const size_t global_col =
+                        static_cast<size_t>(tile_col) * local.tile_size + col;
+                    destination[row * local.tile_size + col] =
+                        global[global_row * local.n + global_col];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void unpackToGlobal(const DistributedMatrix& local,
+                    std::vector<double>& global) {
+    for (int tile_row = local.my_row; tile_row < local.tile_count;
+         tile_row += local.process_rows) {
+        const size_t rows = local.tileRows(tile_row);
+        for (int tile_col = local.my_col; tile_col < local.tile_count;
+             tile_col += local.process_cols) {
+            if (tile_row < tile_col) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t cols = local.tileCols(tile_col);
+            const double* source = local.tile(tile_row, tile_col);
+            for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+                const size_t global_row =
+                    static_cast<size_t>(tile_row) * local.tile_size + row;
+                for (size_t col = 0; col < cols; ++col) {
+                    const size_t global_col =
+                        static_cast<size_t>(tile_col) * local.tile_size + col;
+                    global[global_row * local.n + global_col] =
+                        source[row * local.tile_size + col];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void distributeInitialMatrix(const std::vector<double>& global,
+                             DistributedMatrix& local,
+                             MPI_Comm cartesian,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int root) {
+    const int count = mpiCount(local.values.size());
+
+    if (rank == root) {
+        for (int destination_rank = 0; destination_rank < local.process_rows * local.process_cols;
+             ++destination_rank) {
+            int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+            MPI_Cart_coords(cartesian, destination_rank, 2, coordinates);
+            DistributedMatrix destination(local.n,
+                                          local.tile_size,
+                                          local.process_rows,
+                                          local.process_cols,
+                                          coordinates[0],
+                                          coordinates[1]);
+            packFromGlobal(global, destination);
+
+            const int destination_count = mpiCount(destination.values.size());
+            if (destination_rank == root) {
+                local.values.swap(destination.values);
+            } else {
+                MPI_Send(destination.values.data(),
+                         destination_count,
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         destination_rank,
+                         100,
+                         cartesian);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        MPI_Recv(local.values.data(),
+                 count,
+                 MPI_DOUBLE,
+                 root,
+                 100,
+                 cartesian,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Barrier(cartesian);
+}
+
+void factorDiagonal(double* diagonal,
+                    const size_t block_size,
+                    const int leading_dimension,
+                    bool& success) {
+    success = true;
+
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < block_size; ++row) {
+        for (size_t col = 0; col <= row; ++col) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < col; ++k) {
+                sum += diagonal[row * leading_dimension + k] *
+                       diagonal[col * leading_dimension + k];
+            }
+
+            if (row == col) {
+                const double value =
+                    diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] - sum;
+                if (value <= 0.0) {
+                    success = false;
+                    return;
+                }
+                diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] = sqrt(value);
+            } else {
+                diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] =
+                    (diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] - sum) /
+                    diagonal[col * leading_dimension + col];
+            }
+        }
+
+        for (size_t col = row + 1; col < static_cast<size_t>(leading_dimension);
+             ++col) {
+            diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] = 0.0;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Solve X * L^T = B for each row of B.  L is lower triangular and both B and
+// L are stored row-major with a fixed tile leading dimension.
+void triangularSolve(double* panel_tile,
+                     const double* diagonal,
+                     const size_t rows,
+                     const size_t columns,
+                     const int leading_dimension) {
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        for (size_t col = 0; col < columns; ++col) {
+            double value = panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + col];
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < col; ++k) {
+                value -= panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + k] *
+                         diagonal[col * leading_dimension + k];
+            }
+            panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + col] =
+                value / diagonal[col * leading_dimension + col];
+        }
+        for (size_t col = columns; col < static_cast<size_t>(leading_dimension);
+             ++col) {
+            panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + col] = 0.0;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// C -= A * B^T.  A and B are transposed panel tiles, so the innermost loop
+// operates on contiguous data in both the update tile and B.  This gives the
+// compiler a simple SIMD-friendly kernel without requiring a BLAS dependency.
+void rankKUpdate(double* C,
+                 const double* A_transposed,
+                 const double* B_transposed,
+                 const size_t rows,
+                 const size_t columns,
+                 const size_t inner,
+                 const int leading_dimension) {
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < inner; ++k) {
+        const double* a = A_transposed + k * leading_dimension;
+        const double* b = B_transposed + k * leading_dimension;
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+            double* c = C + row * leading_dimension;
+            const double scalar = a[row];
+#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
+#pragma GCC ivdep
+#endif
+            for (size_t col = 0; col < columns; ++col) {
+                c[col] -= scalar * b[col];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(DistributedMatrix& matrix,
+                           MPI_Comm cartesian,
+                           MPI_Comm row_communicator,
+                           MPI_Comm column_communicator,
+                           const int rank) {
+    const int tile_count = matrix.tile_count;
+    const int leading_dimension = matrix.tile_size;
+    const size_t tile_elements = matrix.tile_elements;
+    const int root_count = matrix.process_rows * matrix.process_cols;
+
+    std::vector<double> diagonal(tile_elements, 0.0);
+    const size_t max_panel_tiles =
+        tile_count == 0
+            ? 0
+            : (static_cast<size_t>(tile_count) + matrix.process_rows - 1) /
+                  matrix.process_rows;
+    std::vector<double> row_panel(std::max<size_t>(1, max_panel_tiles * tile_elements),
+                                  0.0);
+    std::vector<double> panel(std::max<size_t>(1,
+                                               static_cast<size_t>(tile_count) *
+                                                   tile_elements),
+                              0.0);
+    std::vector<int> panel_counts(matrix.process_rows, 0);
+    std::vector<int> panel_displacements(matrix.process_rows, 0);
+    std::vector<int> panel_offsets(tile_count, 0);
+
+    for (int k = 0; k < tile_count; ++k) {
+        const int diagonal_coordinates[2] = {
+            k % matrix.process_rows, k % matrix.process_cols};
+        int diagonal_owner = 0;
+        MPI_Cart_rank(cartesian, diagonal_coordinates, &diagonal_owner);
+
+        int factor_ok = 1;
+        if (rank == diagonal_owner) {
+            const size_t block_size = matrix.tileRows(k);
+            bool local_ok = true;
+            factorDiagonal(matrix.tile(k, k),
+                           block_size,
+                           leading_dimension,
+                           local_ok);
+            factor_ok = local_ok ? 1 : 0;
+            if (!local_ok) {
+                printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+                       static_cast<size_t>(k) * matrix.tile_size);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Bcast(&factor_ok, 1, MPI_INT, diagonal_owner, cartesian);
+        if (factor_ok == 0) {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        if (rank == diagonal_owner) {
+            std::copy(matrix.tile(k, k),
+                      matrix.tile(k, k) + tile_elements,
+                      diagonal.begin());
+        }
+
+        // Every owner of a tile in block column k needs L(k,k).  Only the
+        // process column containing the diagonal participates in this
+        // broadcast.
+        if (matrix.my_col == k % matrix.process_cols) {
+            MPI_Bcast(diagonal.data(),
+                      mpiCount(tile_elements),
+                      MPI_DOUBLE,
+                      k % matrix.process_rows,
+                      column_communicator);
+
+            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                triangularSolve(matrix.tile(tile_row, k),
+                                diagonal.data(),
+                                matrix.tileRows(tile_row),
+                                matrix.tileCols(k),
+                                leading_dimension);
+            }
+        }
+
+        int total_panel_doubles = 0;
+        for (int process_row = 0; process_row < matrix.process_rows;
+             ++process_row) {
+            panel_displacements[process_row] = total_panel_doubles;
+            int row_tile_count = 0;
+            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows == process_row) {
+                    panel_offsets[tile_row] =
+                        total_panel_doubles + row_tile_count *
+                                                     mpiCount(tile_elements);
+                    ++row_tile_count;
+                }
+            }
+            panel_counts[process_row] = row_tile_count * mpiCount(tile_elements);
+            total_panel_doubles += panel_counts[process_row];
+        }
+
+        std::fill(row_panel.begin(), row_panel.end(), 0.0);
+        if (matrix.my_col == k % matrix.process_cols) {
+            int row_tile_number = 0;
+            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                const size_t rows = matrix.tileRows(tile_row);
+                const size_t columns = matrix.tileCols(k);
+                const double* source = matrix.tile(tile_row, k);
+                double* destination =
+                    row_panel.data() + row_tile_number * tile_elements;
+                for (size_t column = 0; column < columns; ++column) {
+                    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+                        destination[column * leading_dimension + row] =
+                            source[row * leading_dimension + column];
+                    }
+                }
+                ++row_tile_number;
+            }
+        }
+
+        // First make each process row's part of the panel available to every
+        // process column, then allgather those parts down each process column.
+        // After these two collectives every process owns a read-only copy of
+        // the current panel, while the factor itself remains distributed.
+        MPI_Bcast(row_panel.data(),
+                  panel_counts[matrix.my_row],
+                  MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  k % matrix.process_cols,
+                  row_communicator);
+        MPI_Allgatherv(row_panel.data(),
+                       panel_counts[matrix.my_row],
+                       MPI_DOUBLE,
+                       panel.data(),
+                       panel_counts.data(),
+                       panel_displacements.data(),
+                       MPI_DOUBLE,
+                       column_communicator);
+
+        for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+            if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t rows = matrix.tileRows(tile_row);
+            const double* left_panel = panel.data() + panel_offsets[tile_row];
+            for (int tile_col = k + 1; tile_col <= tile_row; ++tile_col) {
+                if (tile_col % matrix.process_cols != matrix.my_col) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                rankKUpdate(matrix.tile(tile_row, tile_col),
+                            left_panel,
+                            panel.data() + panel_offsets[tile_col],
+                            rows,
+                            matrix.tileCols(tile_col),
+                            matrix.tileCols(k),
+                            leading_dimension);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    (void)root_count;
+    return true;
+}
+
+void gatherResult(const DistributedMatrix& local,
+                  std::vector<double>& global,
+                  MPI_Comm cartesian,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int root) {
+    const int process_count = local.process_rows * local.process_cols;
+    const int local_count = mpiCount(local.values.size());
+
+    if (rank == root) {
+        std::fill(global.begin(), global.end(), 0.0);
+        unpackToGlobal(local, global);
+
+        for (int source_rank = 0; source_rank < process_count; ++source_rank) {
+            if (source_rank == root) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+            MPI_Cart_coords(cartesian, source_rank, 2, coordinates);
+            DistributedMatrix source(local.n,
+                                     local.tile_size,
+                                     local.process_rows,
+                                     local.process_cols,
+                                     coordinates[0],
+                                     coordinates[1]);
+            MPI_Recv(source.values.data(),
+                     mpiCount(source.values.size()),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     source_rank,
+                     200,
+                     cartesian,
+                     MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            unpackToGlobal(source, global);
+        }
+    } else {
+        MPI_Send(local.values.data(),
+                 local_count,
+                 MPI_DOUBLE,
+                 root,
+                 200,
+                 cartesian);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Barrier(cartesian);
+}

+bool parseSize(const char* text, size_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(
+                     std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

The MPI implementation is now in place in the existing source and CMake files. It uses rank-zero input generation followed by distributed lower-triangular storage, and gathers the result only when `-r` or `-v` requires the original full-matrix behavior. I’m compiling next and will exercise both single-rank and multi-rank runs.

+// A square 2-D block-cyclic distribution gives every process a comparable
+// amount of work and keeps the distributed factor at O(n^2 / P) storage.  A
+// tile is deliberately large enough to make the local rank-k updates compute
+// intensive, while still exposing enough tiles for the process grid.

+struct DistributedMatrix {
+    size_t n = 0;
+    int tile_size = 0;
+    int tile_count = 0;
+    int process_rows = 0;
+    int process_cols = 0;
+    int my_row = 0;
+    int my_col = 0;
+    size_t local_tile_rows = 0;
+    size_t local_tile_cols = 0;
+    size_t tile_elements = 0;
+    std::vector<double> values;
+
+    DistributedMatrix() = default;
+
+    DistributedMatrix(const size_t matrix_size,
+                      const int block_size,
+                      const int grid_rows,
+                      const int grid_cols,
+                      const int process_row,
+                      const int process_col)
+        : n(matrix_size),
+          tile_size(block_size),
+          process_rows(grid_rows),
+          process_cols(grid_cols),
+          my_row(process_row),
+          my_col(process_col),
+          tile_elements(static_cast<size_t>(block_size) * block_size) {
+        const size_t tile_count_size = (n + tile_size - 1) / tile_size;
+        tile_count = static_cast<int>(tile_count_size);
+        local_tile_rows = tile_count <= my_row
+                              ? 0
+                              : 1 + static_cast<size_t>(tile_count - 1 - my_row) /
+                                    process_rows;
+        local_tile_cols = tile_count <= my_col
+                              ? 0
+                              : 1 + static_cast<size_t>(tile_count - 1 - my_col) /
+                                    process_cols;
+        values.resize(local_tile_rows * local_tile_cols * tile_elements, 0.0);
+    }
+
+    size_t tileRows(const int tile_row) const {
+        const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(tile_row) * tile_size;
+        return std::min(static_cast<size_t>(tile_size), n - first);
+    }
+
+    size_t tileCols(const int tile_col) const {
+        const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(tile_col) * tile_size;
+        return std::min(static_cast<size_t>(tile_size), n - first);
+    }
+
+    double* tile(const int tile_row, const int tile_col) {
+        const size_t local_row =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_row - my_row) / process_rows;
+        const size_t local_col =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_col - my_col) / process_cols;
+        const size_t offset =
+            (local_row * local_tile_cols + local_col) * tile_elements;
+        return values.data() + offset;
+    }
+
+    const double* tile(const int tile_row, const int tile_col) const {
+        const size_t local_row =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_row - my_row) / process_rows;
+        const size_t local_col =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_col - my_col) / process_cols;
+        const size_t offset =
+            (local_row * local_tile_cols + local_col) * tile_elements;
+        return values.data() + offset;
+    }
+};
+
+int mpiCount(const size_t count) {
+    if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI message is too large for the MPI count interface\n");
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+    }
+    return static_cast<int>(count);
+}
+
+void packFromGlobal(const std::vector<double>& global,
+                    DistributedMatrix& local) {
+    std::fill(local.values.begin(), local.values.end(), 0.0);
+
+    for (int tile_row = local.my_row; tile_row < local.tile_count;
+         tile_row += local.process_rows) {
+        const size_t rows = local.tileRows(tile_row);
+        for (int tile_col = local.my_col; tile_col < local.tile_count;
+             tile_col += local.process_cols) {
+            if (tile_row < tile_col) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t cols = local.tileCols(tile_col);
+            double* destination = local.tile(tile_row, tile_col);
+            for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+                const size_t global_row =
+                    static_cast<size_t>(tile_row) * local.tile_size + row;
+                for (size_t col = 0; col < cols; ++col) {
+                    const size_t global_col =
+                        static_cast<size_t>(tile_col) * local.tile_size + col;
+                    destination[row * local.tile_size + col] =
+                        global[global_row * local.n + global_col];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void unpackToGlobal(const DistributedMatrix& local,
+                    std::vector<double>& global) {
+    for (int tile_row = local.my_row; tile_row < local.tile_count;
+         tile_row += local.process_rows) {
+        const size_t rows = local.tileRows(tile_row);
+        for (int tile_col = local.my_col; tile_col < local.tile_count;
+             tile_col += local.process_cols) {
+            if (tile_row < tile_col) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t cols = local.tileCols(tile_col);
+            const double* source = local.tile(tile_row, tile_col);
+            for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+                const size_t global_row =
+                    static_cast<size_t>(tile_row) * local.tile_size + row;
+                for (size_t col = 0; col < cols; ++col) {
+                    const size_t global_col =
+                        static_cast<size_t>(tile_col) * local.tile_size + col;
+                    global[global_row * local.n + global_col] =
+                        source[row * local.tile_size + col];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void distributeInitialMatrix(const std::vector<double>& global,
+                             DistributedMatrix& local,
+                             MPI_Comm cartesian,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int root) {
+    const int count = mpiCount(local.values.size());
+
+    if (rank == root) {
+        for (int destination_rank = 0; destination_rank < local.process_rows * local.process_cols;
+             ++destination_rank) {
+            int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+            MPI_Cart_coords(cartesian, destination_rank, 2, coordinates);
+            DistributedMatrix destination(local.n,
+                                          local.tile_size,
+                                          local.process_rows,
+                                          local.process_cols,
+                                          coordinates[0],
+                                          coordinates[1]);
+            packFromGlobal(global, destination);
+
+            const int destination_count = mpiCount(destination.values.size());
+            if (destination_rank == root) {
+                local.values.swap(destination.values);
+            } else {
+                MPI_Send(destination.values.data(),
+                         destination_count,
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         destination_rank,
+                         100,
+                         cartesian);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        MPI_Recv(local.values.data(),
+                 count,
+                 MPI_DOUBLE,
+                 root,
+                 100,
+                 cartesian,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Barrier(cartesian);
+}
+
+void factorDiagonal(double* diagonal,
+                    const size_t block_size,
+                    const int leading_dimension,
+                    bool& success) {
+    success = true;
+
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < block_size; ++row) {
+        for (size_t col = 0; col <= row; ++col) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < col; ++k) {
+                sum += diagonal[row * leading_dimension + k] *
+                       diagonal[col * leading_dimension + k];
+            }
+
+            if (row == col) {
+                const double value =
+                    diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] - sum;
+                if (value <= 0.0) {
+                    success = false;
+                    return;
+                }
+                diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] = sqrt(value);
+            } else {
+                diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] =
+                    (diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] - sum) /
+                    diagonal[col * leading_dimension + col];
+            }
+        }
+
+        for (size_t col = row + 1; col < static_cast<size_t>(leading_dimension);
+             ++col) {
+            diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] = 0.0;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Solve X * L^T = B for each row of B.  L is lower triangular and both B and
+// L are stored row-major with a fixed tile leading dimension.
+void triangularSolve(double* panel_tile,
+                     const double* diagonal,
+                     const size_t rows,
+                     const size_t columns,
+                     const int leading_dimension) {
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        for (size_t col = 0; col < columns; ++col) {
+            double value = panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + col];
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < col; ++k) {
+                value -= panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + k] *
+                         diagonal[col * leading_dimension + k];
+            }
+            panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + col] =
+                value / diagonal[col * leading_dimension + col];
+        }
+        for (size_t col = columns; col < static_cast<size_t>(leading_dimension);
+             ++col) {
+            panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + col] = 0.0;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// C -= A * B^T.  A and B are transposed panel tiles, so the innermost loop
+// operates on contiguous data in both the update tile and B.  This gives the
+// compiler a simple SIMD-friendly kernel without requiring a BLAS dependency.
+void rankKUpdate(double* C,
+                 const double* A_transposed,
+                 const double* B_transposed,
+                 const size_t rows,
+                 const size_t columns,
+                 const size_t inner,
+                 const int leading_dimension) {
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < inner; ++k) {
+        const double* a = A_transposed + k * leading_dimension;
+        const double* b = B_transposed + k * leading_dimension;
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+            double* c = C + row * leading_dimension;
+            const double scalar = a[row];
+#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
+#pragma GCC ivdep
+#endif
+            for (size_t col = 0; col < columns; ++col) {
+                c[col] -= scalar * b[col];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(DistributedMatrix& matrix,
+                           MPI_Comm cartesian,
+                           MPI_Comm row_communicator,
+                           MPI_Comm column_communicator,
+                           const int rank) {
+    const int tile_count = matrix.tile_count;
+    const int leading_dimension = matrix.tile_size;
+    const size_t tile_elements = matrix.tile_elements;
+    const int root_count = matrix.process_rows * matrix.process_cols;
+
+    std::vector<double> diagonal(tile_elements, 0.0);
+    const size_t max_panel_tiles =
+        tile_count == 0
+            ? 0
+            : (static_cast<size_t>(tile_count) + matrix.process_rows - 1) /
+                  matrix.process_rows;
+    std::vector<double> row_panel(std::max<size_t>(1, max_panel_tiles * tile_elements),
+                                  0.0);
+    std::vector<double> panel(std::max<size_t>(1,
+                                               static_cast<size_t>(tile_count) *
+                                                   tile_elements),
+                              0.0);
+    std::vector<int> panel_counts(matrix.process_rows, 0);
+    std::vector<int> panel_displacements(matrix.process_rows, 0);
+    std::vector<int> panel_offsets(tile_count, 0);
+
+    for (int k = 0; k < tile_count; ++k) {
+        const int diagonal_coordinates[2] = {
+            k % matrix.process_rows, k % matrix.process_cols};
+        int diagonal_owner = 0;
+        MPI_Cart_rank(cartesian, diagonal_coordinates, &diagonal_owner);
+
+        int factor_ok = 1;
+        if (rank == diagonal_owner) {
+            const size_t block_size = matrix.tileRows(k);
+            bool local_ok = true;
+            factorDiagonal(matrix.tile(k, k),
+                           block_size,
+                           leading_dimension,
+                           local_ok);
+            factor_ok = local_ok ? 1 : 0;
+            if (!local_ok) {
+                printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+                       static_cast<size_t>(k) * matrix.tile_size);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Bcast(&factor_ok, 1, MPI_INT, diagonal_owner, cartesian);
+        if (factor_ok == 0) {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        if (rank == diagonal_owner) {
+            std::copy(matrix.tile(k, k),
+                      matrix.tile(k, k) + tile_elements,
+                      diagonal.begin());
+        }
+
+        // Every owner of a tile in block column k needs L(k,k).  Only the
+        // process column containing the diagonal participates in this
+        // broadcast.
+        if (matrix.my_col == k % matrix.process_cols) {
+            MPI_Bcast(diagonal.data(),
+                      mpiCount(tile_elements),
+                      MPI_DOUBLE,
+                      k % matrix.process_rows,
+                      column_communicator);
+
+            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                triangularSolve(matrix.tile(tile_row, k),
+                                diagonal.data(),
+                                matrix.tileRows(tile_row),
+                                matrix.tileCols(k),
+                                leading_dimension);
+            }
+        }
+
+        int total_panel_doubles = 0;
+        for (int process_row = 0; process_row < matrix.process_rows;
+             ++process_row) {
+            panel_displacements[process_row] = total_panel_doubles;
+            int row_tile_count = 0;
+            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows == process_row) {
+                    panel_offsets[tile_row] =
+                        total_panel_doubles + row_tile_count *
+                                                     mpiCount(tile_elements);
+                    ++row_tile_count;
+                }
+            }
+            panel_counts[process_row] = row_tile_count * mpiCount(tile_elements);
+            total_panel_doubles += panel_counts[process_row];
+        }
+
+        std::fill(row_panel.begin(), row_panel.end(), 0.0);
+        if (matrix.my_col == k % matrix.process_cols) {
+            int row_tile_number = 0;
+            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                const size_t rows = matrix.tileRows(tile_row);
+                const size_t columns = matrix.tileCols(k);
+                const double* source = matrix.tile(tile_row, k);
+                double* destination =
+                    row_panel.data() + row_tile_number * tile_elements;
+                for (size_t column = 0; column < columns; ++column) {
+                    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+                        destination[column * leading_dimension + row] =
+                            source[row * leading_dimension + column];
+                    }
+                }
+                ++row_tile_number;
+            }
+        }
+
+        // First make each process row's part of the panel available to every
+        // process column, then allgather those parts down each process column.
+        // After these two collectives every process owns a read-only copy of
+        // the current panel, while the factor itself remains distributed.
+        MPI_Bcast(row_panel.data(),
+                  panel_counts[matrix.my_row],
+                  MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  k % matrix.process_cols,
+                  row_communicator);
+        MPI_Allgatherv(row_panel.data(),
+                       panel_counts[matrix.my_row],
+                       MPI_DOUBLE,
+                       panel.data(),
+                       panel_counts.data(),
+                       panel_displacements.data(),
+                       MPI_DOUBLE,
+                       column_communicator);
+
+        for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+            if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t rows = matrix.tileRows(tile_row);
+            const double* left_panel = panel.data() + panel_offsets[tile_row];
+            for (int tile_col = k + 1; tile_col <= tile_row; ++tile_col) {
+                if (tile_col % matrix.process_cols != matrix.my_col) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                rankKUpdate(matrix.tile(tile_row, tile_col),
+                            left_panel,
+                            panel.data() + panel_offsets[tile_col],
+                            rows,
+                            matrix.tileCols(tile_col),
+                            matrix.tileCols(k),
+                            leading_dimension);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    (void)root_count;
+    return true;
+}
+
+void gatherResult(const DistributedMatrix& local,
+                  std::vector<double>& global,
+                  MPI_Comm cartesian,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int root) {
+    const int process_count = local.process_rows * local.process_cols;
+    const int local_count = mpiCount(local.values.size());
+
+    if (rank == root) {
+        std::fill(global.begin(), global.end(), 0.0);
+        unpackToGlobal(local, global);
+
+        for (int source_rank = 0; source_rank < process_count; ++source_rank) {
+            if (source_rank == root) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+            MPI_Cart_coords(cartesian, source_rank, 2, coordinates);
+            DistributedMatrix source(local.n,
+                                     local.tile_size,
+                                     local.process_rows,
+                                     local.process_cols,
+                                     coordinates[0],
+                                     coordinates[1]);
+            MPI_Recv(source.values.data(),
+                     mpiCount(source.values.size()),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     source_rank,
+                     200,
+                     cartesian,
+                     MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            unpackToGlobal(source, global);
+        }
+    } else {
+        MPI_Send(local.values.data(),
+                 local_count,
+                 MPI_DOUBLE,
+                 root,
+                 200,
+                 cartesian);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Barrier(cartesian);
+}
+

     printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
     printf("Options:\n");

     printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
 }
 
+bool parseSize(const char* text, size_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(
+                     std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

+// tile is deliberately large enough to make the local rank-k updates compute
+// intensive, while still exposing enough tiles for the process grid.
