+struct DistributedMatrix {
+    size_t n = 0;
+    int tile_size = 0;
+    int tile_count = 0;
+    int process_rows = 0;
+    int process_cols = 0;
+    int my_row = 0;
+    int my_col = 0;
+    size_t local_tile_rows = 0;
+    size_t local_tile_cols = 0;
+    size_t tile_elements = 0;
+    std::vector<double> values;
+
+    DistributedMatrix() = default;
+
+    DistributedMatrix(const size_t matrix_size,
+                      const int block_size,
+                      const int grid_rows,
+                      const int grid_cols,
+                      const int process_row,
+                      const int process_col)
+        : n(matrix_size),
+          tile_size(block_size),
+          process_rows(grid_rows),
+          process_cols(grid_cols),
+          my_row(process_row),
+          my_col(process_col),
+          tile_elements(static_cast<size_t>(block_size) * block_size) {
+        const size_t tile_count_size = (n + tile_size - 1) / tile_size;
+        tile_count = static_cast<int>(tile_count_size);
+        local_tile_rows = tile_count <= my_row
+                              ? 0
+                              : 1 + static_cast<size_t>(tile_count - 1 - my_row) /
+                                    process_rows;
+        local_tile_cols = tile_count <= my_col
+                              ? 0
+                              : 1 + static_cast<size_t>(tile_count - 1 - my_col) /
+                                    process_cols;
+        values.resize(local_tile_rows * local_tile_cols * tile_elements, 0.0);
+    }
+
+    size_t tileRows(const int tile_row) const {
+        const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(tile_row) * tile_size;
+        return std::min(static_cast<size_t>(tile_size), n - first);
+    }
+
+    size_t tileCols(const int tile_col) const {
+        const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(tile_col) * tile_size;
+        return std::min(static_cast<size_t>(tile_size), n - first);
+    }
+
+    double* tile(const int tile_row, const int tile_col) {
+        const size_t local_row =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_row - my_row) / process_rows;
+        const size_t local_col =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_col - my_col) / process_cols;
+        const size_t offset =
+            (local_row * local_tile_cols + local_col) * tile_elements;
+        return values.data() + offset;
+    }
+
+    const double* tile(const int tile_row, const int tile_col) const {
+        const size_t local_row =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_row - my_row) / process_rows;
+        const size_t local_col =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_col - my_col) / process_cols;
+        const size_t offset =
+            (local_row * local_tile_cols + local_col) * tile_elements;
+        return values.data() + offset;
+    }
+};
+
+int mpiCount(const size_t count) {
+    if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI message is too large for the MPI count interface\n");
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+    }
+    return static_cast<int>(count);
+}
+
+void packFromGlobal(const std::vector<double>& global,
+                    DistributedMatrix& local) {
+    std::fill(local.values.begin(), local.values.end(), 0.0);
+
+    for (int tile_row = local.my_row; tile_row < local.tile_count;
+         tile_row += local.process_rows) {
+        const size_t rows = local.tileRows(tile_row);
+        for (int tile_col = local.my_col; tile_col < local.tile_count;
+             tile_col += local.process_cols) {
+            if (tile_row < tile_col) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t cols = local.tileCols(tile_col);
+            double* destination = local.tile(tile_row, tile_col);
+            for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+                const size_t global_row =
+                    static_cast<size_t>(tile_row) * local.tile_size + row;
+                for (size_t col = 0; col < cols; ++col) {
+                    const size_t global_col =
+                        static_cast<size_t>(tile_col) * local.tile_size + col;
+                    destination[row * local.tile_size + col] =
+                        global[global_row * local.n + global_col];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void unpackToGlobal(const DistributedMatrix& local,
+                    std::vector<double>& global) {
+    for (int tile_row = local.my_row; tile_row < local.tile_count;
+         tile_row += local.process_rows) {
+        const size_t rows = local.tileRows(tile_row);
+        for (int tile_col = local.my_col; tile_col < local.tile_count;
+             tile_col += local.process_cols) {
+            if (tile_row < tile_col) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t cols = local.tileCols(tile_col);
+            const double* source = local.tile(tile_row, tile_col);
+            for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+                const size_t global_row =
+                    static_cast<size_t>(tile_row) * local.tile_size + row;
+                for (size_t col = 0; col < cols; ++col) {
+                    const size_t global_col =
+                        static_cast<size_t>(tile_col) * local.tile_size + col;
+                    global[global_row * local.n + global_col] =
+                        source[row * local.tile_size + col];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void distributeInitialMatrix(const std::vector<double>& global,
+                             DistributedMatrix& local,
+                             MPI_Comm cartesian,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int root) {
+    const int count = mpiCount(local.values.size());
+
+    if (rank == root) {
+        for (int destination_rank = 0; destination_rank < local.process_rows * local.process_cols;
+             ++destination_rank) {
+            int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+            MPI_Cart_coords(cartesian, destination_rank, 2, coordinates);
+            DistributedMatrix destination(local.n,
+                                          local.tile_size,
+                                          local.process_rows,
+                                          local.process_cols,
+                                          coordinates[0],
+                                          coordinates[1]);
+            packFromGlobal(global, destination);
+
+            const int destination_count = mpiCount(destination.values.size());
+            if (destination_rank == root) {
+                local.values.swap(destination.values);
+            } else {
+                MPI_Send(destination.values.data(),
+                         destination_count,
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         destination_rank,
+                         100,
+                         cartesian);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        MPI_Recv(local.values.data(),
+                 count,
+                 MPI_DOUBLE,
+                 root,
+                 100,
+                 cartesian,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Barrier(cartesian);
+}
+
+void factorDiagonal(double* diagonal,
+                    const size_t block_size,
+                    const int leading_dimension,
+                    bool& success) {
+    success = true;
+
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < block_size; ++row) {
+        for (size_t col = 0; col <= row; ++col) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < col; ++k) {
+                sum += diagonal[row * leading_dimension + k] *
+                       diagonal[col * leading_dimension + k];
+            }
+
+            if (row == col) {
+                const double value =
+                    diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] - sum;
+                if (value <= 0.0) {
+                    success = false;
+                    return;
+                }
+                diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] = sqrt(value);
+            } else {
+                diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] =
+                    (diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] - sum) /
+                    diagonal[col * leading_dimension + col];
+            }
+        }
+
+        for (size_t col = row + 1; col < static_cast<size_t>(leading_dimension);
+             ++col) {
+            diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] = 0.0;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Solve X * L^T = B for each row of B.  L is lower triangular and both B and
+// L are stored row-major with a fixed tile leading dimension.
+void triangularSolve(double* panel_tile,
+                     const double* diagonal,
+                     const size_t rows,
+                     const size_t columns,
+                     const int leading_dimension) {
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        for (size_t col = 0; col < columns; ++col) {
+            double value = panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + col];
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < col; ++k) {
+                value -= panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + k] *
+                         diagonal[col * leading_dimension + k];
+            }
+            panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + col] =
+                value / diagonal[col * leading_dimension + col];
+        }
+        for (size_t col = columns; col < static_cast<size_t>(leading_dimension);
+             ++col) {
+            panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + col] = 0.0;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// C -= A * B^T.  A and B are transposed panel tiles, so the innermost loop
+// operates on contiguous data in both the update tile and B.  This gives the
+// compiler a simple SIMD-friendly kernel without requiring a BLAS dependency.
+void rankKUpdate(double* C,
+                 const double* A_transposed,
+                 const double* B_transposed,
+                 const size_t rows,
+                 const size_t columns,
+                 const size_t inner,
+                 const int leading_dimension) {
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < inner; ++k) {
+        const double* a = A_transposed + k * leading_dimension;
+        const double* b = B_transposed + k * leading_dimension;
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+            double* c = C + row * leading_dimension;
+            const double scalar = a[row];
+#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
+#pragma GCC ivdep
+#endif
+            for (size_t col = 0; col < columns; ++col) {
+                c[col] -= scalar * b[col];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(DistributedMatrix& matrix,
+                           MPI_Comm cartesian,
+                           MPI_Comm row_communicator,
+                           MPI_Comm column_communicator,
+                           const int rank) {
+    const int tile_count = matrix.tile_count;
+    const int leading_dimension = matrix.tile_size;
+    const size_t tile_elements = matrix.tile_elements;
+
+    std::vector<double> diagonal(tile_elements, 0.0);
+    const size_t max_panel_tiles =
+        tile_count == 0
+            ? 0
+            : (static_cast<size_t>(tile_count) + matrix.process_rows - 1) /
+                  matrix.process_rows;
+    std::vector<double> row_panel(std::max<size_t>(1, max_panel_tiles * tile_elements),
+                                  0.0);
+    std::vector<double> panel(std::max<size_t>(1,
+                                               static_cast<size_t>(tile_count) *
+                                                   tile_elements),
+                              0.0);
+    std::vector<int> panel_counts(matrix.process_rows, 0);
+    std::vector<int> panel_displacements(matrix.process_rows, 0);
+    std::vector<int> panel_offsets(tile_count, 0);
+
+    for (int k = 0; k < tile_count; ++k) {
+        const int diagonal_coordinates[2] = {
+            k % matrix.process_rows, k % matrix.process_cols};
+        int diagonal_owner = 0;
+        MPI_Cart_rank(cartesian, diagonal_coordinates, &diagonal_owner);
+
+        int factor_ok = 1;
+        if (rank == diagonal_owner) {
+            const size_t block_size = matrix.tileRows(k);
+            bool local_ok = true;
+            factorDiagonal(matrix.tile(k, k),
+                           block_size,
+                           leading_dimension,
+                           local_ok);
+            factor_ok = local_ok ? 1 : 0;
+            if (!local_ok) {
+                printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+                       static_cast<size_t>(k) * matrix.tile_size);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Bcast(&factor_ok, 1, MPI_INT, diagonal_owner, cartesian);
+        if (factor_ok == 0) {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        if (rank == diagonal_owner) {
+            std::copy(matrix.tile(k, k),
+                      matrix.tile(k, k) + tile_elements,
+                      diagonal.begin());
+        }
+
+        // Every owner of a tile in block column k needs L(k,k).  Only the
+        // process column containing the diagonal participates in this
+        // broadcast.
+        if (matrix.my_col == k % matrix.process_cols) {
+            MPI_Bcast(diagonal.data(),
+                      mpiCount(tile_elements),
+                      MPI_DOUBLE,
+                      k % matrix.process_rows,
+                      column_communicator);
+
+            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                triangularSolve(matrix.tile(tile_row, k),
+                                diagonal.data(),
+                                matrix.tileRows(tile_row),
+                                matrix.tileCols(k),
+                                leading_dimension);
+            }
+        }
+
+        int total_panel_doubles = 0;
+        for (int process_row = 0; process_row < matrix.process_rows;
+             ++process_row) {
+            panel_displacements[process_row] = total_panel_doubles;
+            int row_tile_count = 0;
+            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows == process_row) {
+                    panel_offsets[tile_row] =
+                        total_panel_doubles + row_tile_count *
+                                                     mpiCount(tile_elements);
+                    ++row_tile_count;
+                }
+            }
+            panel_counts[process_row] = row_tile_count * mpiCount(tile_elements);
+            total_panel_doubles += panel_counts[process_row];
+        }
+
+        std::fill(row_panel.begin(), row_panel.end(), 0.0);
+        if (matrix.my_col == k % matrix.process_cols) {
+            int row_tile_number = 0;
+            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                const size_t rows = matrix.tileRows(tile_row);
+                const size_t columns = matrix.tileCols(k);
+                const double* source = matrix.tile(tile_row, k);
+                double* destination =
+                    row_panel.data() + row_tile_number * tile_elements;
+                for (size_t column = 0; column < columns; ++column) {
+                    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+                        destination[column * leading_dimension + row] =
+                            source[row * leading_dimension + column];
+                    }
+                }
+                ++row_tile_number;
+            }
+        }
+
+        // First make each process row's part of the panel available to every
+        // process column, then allgather those parts down each process column.
+        // After these two collectives every process owns a read-only copy of
+        // the current panel, while the factor itself remains distributed.
+        MPI_Bcast(row_panel.data(),
+                  panel_counts[matrix.my_row],
+                  MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  k % matrix.process_cols,
+                  row_communicator);
+        MPI_Allgatherv(row_panel.data(),
+                       panel_counts[matrix.my_row],
+                       MPI_DOUBLE,
+                       panel.data(),
+                       panel_counts.data(),
+                       panel_displacements.data(),
+                       MPI_DOUBLE,
+                       column_communicator);
+
+        for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+            if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t rows = matrix.tileRows(tile_row);
+            const double* left_panel = panel.data() + panel_offsets[tile_row];
+            for (int tile_col = k + 1; tile_col <= tile_row; ++tile_col) {
+                if (tile_col % matrix.process_cols != matrix.my_col) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                rankKUpdate(matrix.tile(tile_row, tile_col),
+                            left_panel,
+                            panel.data() + panel_offsets[tile_col],
+                            rows,
+                            matrix.tileCols(tile_col),
+                            matrix.tileCols(k),
+                            leading_dimension);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
     return true;
 }
 
+void gatherResult(const DistributedMatrix& local,
+                  std::vector<double>& global,
+                  MPI_Comm cartesian,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int root) {
+    const int process_count = local.process_rows * local.process_cols;
+    const int local_count = mpiCount(local.values.size());
+
+    if (rank == root) {
+        std::fill(global.begin(), global.end(), 0.0);
+        unpackToGlobal(local, global);
+
+        for (int source_rank = 0; source_rank < process_count; ++source_rank) {
+            if (source_rank == root) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+            MPI_Cart_coords(cartesian, source_rank, 2, coordinates);
+            DistributedMatrix source(local.n,
+                                     local.tile_size,
+                                     local.process_rows,
+                                     local.process_cols,
+                                     coordinates[0],
+                                     coordinates[1]);
+            MPI_Recv(source.values.data(),
+                     mpiCount(source.values.size()),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     source_rank,
+                     200,
+                     cartesian,
+                     MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            unpackToGlobal(source, global);
+        }
+    } else {
+        MPI_Send(local.values.data(),
+                 local_count,
+                 MPI_DOUBLE,
+                 root,
+                 200,
+                 cartesian);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Barrier(cartesian);
+}

     printf("Options:\n");

+bool parseSize(const char* text, size_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(
+                     std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

    14	// A square 2-D block-cyclic distribution gives every process a comparable
    15	// amount of work and keeps the distributed factor at O(n^2 / P) storage.  A
    16	// tile is deliberately large enough to make the local rank-k updates compute
    17	// intensive, while still exposing enough tiles for the process grid.
    18	constexpr int kTileSize = 128;
    19	
    20	// Generate the same symmetric positive definite matrix as the original
    21	// benchmark.  The matrix is generated by rank zero and distributed before
    22	// timing the factorization; this retains the benchmark's input semantics
    23	// without replicating the working matrix on every process.
    24	void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    25	    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    26	    unsigned int seed = 42;
    27	
    28	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
    29	        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    30	    }
    31	
    32	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
    33	        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
    34	            double sum = 0.0;
    35	            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
    36	                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
    37	            }
    38	            A[i * n + j] = sum;
    39	        }
    40	    }
   100	                      const int block_size,
   101	                      const int grid_rows,
   102	                      const int grid_cols,
   103	                      const int process_row,
   104	                      const int process_col)
   105	        : n(matrix_size),
   106	          tile_size(block_size),
   107	          process_rows(grid_rows),
   108	          process_cols(grid_cols),
   109	          my_row(process_row),
   110	          my_col(process_col),
   111	          tile_elements(static_cast<size_t>(block_size) * block_size) {
   112	        const size_t tile_count_size = (n + tile_size - 1) / tile_size;
   113	        tile_count = static_cast<int>(tile_count_size);
   114	        local_tile_rows = tile_count <= my_row
   115	                              ? 0
   116	                              : 1 + static_cast<size_t>(tile_count - 1 - my_row) /
   117	                                    process_rows;
   118	        local_tile_cols = tile_count <= my_col
   119	                              ? 0
   120	                              : 1 + static_cast<size_t>(tile_count - 1 - my_col) /
   121	                                    process_cols;
   122	        values.resize(local_tile_rows * local_tile_cols * tile_elements, 0.0);
   123	    }
   124	
   125	    size_t tileRows(const int tile_row) const {
   126	        const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(tile_row) * tile_size;
   127	        return std::min(static_cast<size_t>(tile_size), n - first);
   128	    }
   129	
   130	    size_t tileCols(const int tile_col) const {
   131	        const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(tile_col) * tile_size;
   132	        return std::min(static_cast<size_t>(tile_size), n - first);
   133	    }
   134	
   135	    double* tile(const int tile_row, const int tile_col) {
   136	        const size_t local_row =
   137	            static_cast<size_t>(tile_row - my_row) / process_rows;
   138	        const size_t local_col =
   139	            static_cast<size_t>(tile_col - my_col) / process_cols;
   140	        const size_t offset =
   141	            (local_row * local_tile_cols + local_col) * tile_elements;
   142	        return values.data() + offset;
   143	    }
   144	
   145	    const double* tile(const int tile_row, const int tile_col) const {
   146	        const size_t local_row =
   147	            static_cast<size_t>(tile_row - my_row) / process_rows;
   148	        const size_t local_col =
   149	            static_cast<size_t>(tile_col - my_col) / process_cols;
   150	        const size_t offset =
   151	            (local_row * local_tile_cols + local_col) * tile_elements;
   152	        return values.data() + offset;
   153	    }
   154	};
   155	
   156	int mpiCount(const size_t count) {
   157	    if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   158	        fprintf(stderr, "MPI message is too large for the MPI count interface\n");
   159	        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
   160	    }
   161	    return static_cast<int>(count);
   162	}

   164	void packFromGlobal(const std::vector<double>& global,
   165	                    DistributedMatrix& local) {

   169	         tile_row += local.process_rows) {

   330	                 const double* B_transposed,
   331	                 const size_t rows,
   332	                 const size_t columns,
   333	                 const size_t inner,
   334	                 const int leading_dimension) {
   335	    for (size_t k = 0; k < inner; ++k) {
   336	        const double* a = A_transposed + k * leading_dimension;
   337	        const double* b = B_transposed + k * leading_dimension;
   338	        for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
   339	            double* c = C + row * leading_dimension;
   340	            const double scalar = a[row];
   341	#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
   342	#pragma GCC ivdep
   343	#endif
   344	            for (size_t col = 0; col < columns; ++col) {
   345	                c[col] -= scalar * b[col];
   346	            }
   347	        }
   348	    }

   351	bool choleskyDecomposition(DistributedMatrix& matrix,
   352	                           MPI_Comm cartesian,
   353	                           MPI_Comm row_communicator,
   354	                           MPI_Comm column_communicator,
   355	                           const int rank) {
   356	    const int tile_count = matrix.tile_count;
   357	    const int leading_dimension = matrix.tile_size;
   358	    const size_t tile_elements = matrix.tile_elements;
   359	
   360	    std::vector<double> diagonal(tile_elements, 0.0);
   361	    const size_t max_panel_tiles =
   362	        tile_count == 0
   363	            ? 0
   364	            : (static_cast<size_t>(tile_count) + matrix.process_rows - 1) /
   365	                  matrix.process_rows;
   366	    std::vector<double> row_panel(std::max<size_t>(1, max_panel_tiles * tile_elements),
   367	                                  0.0);
   368	    std::vector<double> panel(std::max<size_t>(1,
   369	                                               static_cast<size_t>(tile_count) *
   370	                                                   tile_elements),
   371	                              0.0);
   372	    std::vector<int> panel_counts(matrix.process_rows, 0);
   373	    std::vector<int> panel_displacements(matrix.process_rows, 0);
   374	    std::vector<int> panel_offsets(tile_count, 0);
   375	
   376	    for (int k = 0; k < tile_count; ++k) {
   377	        const int diagonal_coordinates[2] = {
   378	            k % matrix.process_rows, k % matrix.process_cols};
   379	        int diagonal_owner = 0;
   380	        MPI_Cart_rank(cartesian, diagonal_coordinates, &diagonal_owner);
   381	
   382	        int factor_ok = 1;
   383	        if (rank == diagonal_owner) {
   384	            const size_t block_size = matrix.tileRows(k);
   385	            bool local_ok = true;
   386	            factorDiagonal(matrix.tile(k, k),
   387	                           block_size,
   388	                           leading_dimension,
   389	                           local_ok);
   390	            factor_ok = local_ok ? 1 : 0;
   391	            if (!local_ok) {
   392	                printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
   393	                       static_cast<size_t>(k) * matrix.tile_size);
   394	            }
   395	        }
   396	        MPI_Bcast(&factor_ok, 1, MPI_INT, diagonal_owner, cartesian);
   397	        if (factor_ok == 0) {
   398	            return false;
   399	        }
   400	
   401	        if (rank == diagonal_owner) {
   402	            std::copy(matrix.tile(k, k),
   403	                      matrix.tile(k, k) + tile_elements,
   404	                      diagonal.begin());
   405	        }
   406	
   407	        // Every owner of a tile in block column k needs L(k,k).  Only the
   408	        // process column containing the diagonal participates in this
   409	        // broadcast.
   410	        if (matrix.my_col == k % matrix.process_cols) {
   411	            MPI_Bcast(diagonal.data(),
   412	                      mpiCount(tile_elements),
   413	                      MPI_DOUBLE,
   414	                      k % matrix.process_rows,
   415	                      column_communicator);
   416	
   417	            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
   418	                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
   419	                    continue;
   420	                }
   421	                triangularSolve(matrix.tile(tile_row, k),
   422	                                diagonal.data(),
   423	                                matrix.tileRows(tile_row),
   424	                                matrix.tileCols(k),
   425	                                leading_dimension);
   426	            }
   427	        }
   428	
   429	        int total_panel_doubles = 0;
   430	        for (int process_row = 0; process_row < matrix.process_rows;
   431	             ++process_row) {
   432	            panel_displacements[process_row] = total_panel_doubles;
   433	            int row_tile_count = 0;
   434	            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
   435	                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows == process_row) {
   436	                    panel_offsets[tile_row] =
   437	                        total_panel_doubles + row_tile_count *
   438	                                                     mpiCount(tile_elements);
   439	                    ++row_tile_count;
   440	                }
   441	            }
   442	            panel_counts[process_row] = row_tile_count * mpiCount(tile_elements);
   443	            total_panel_doubles += panel_counts[process_row];
   444	        }
   445	
   446	        std::fill(row_panel.begin(), row_panel.end(), 0.0);
   447	        if (matrix.my_col == k % matrix.process_cols) {
   448	            int row_tile_number = 0;
   449	            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
   450	                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
   451	                    continue;
   452	                }
   453	
   454	                const size_t rows = matrix.tileRows(tile_row);
   455	                const size_t columns = matrix.tileCols(k);
   456	                const double* source = matrix.tile(tile_row, k);
   457	                double* destination =
   458	                    row_panel.data() + row_tile_number * tile_elements;
   459	                for (size_t column = 0; column < columns; ++column) {
   460	                    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
   461	                        destination[column * leading_dimension + row] =
   462	                            source[row * leading_dimension + column];
   463	                    }
   464	                }
   465	                ++row_tile_number;
   466	            }
   467	        }
   468	
   469	        // First make each process row's part of the panel available to every
   470	        // process column, then allgather those parts down each process column.
   471	        // After these two collectives every process owns a read-only copy of
   472	        // the current panel, while the factor itself remains distributed.
   473	        MPI_Bcast(row_panel.data(),
   474	                  panel_counts[matrix.my_row],
   475	                  MPI_DOUBLE,
   476	                  k % matrix.process_cols,
   477	                  row_communicator);
   478	        MPI_Allgatherv(row_panel.data(),
   479	                       panel_counts[matrix.my_row],
   480	                       MPI_DOUBLE,
   481	                       panel.data(),
   482	                       panel_counts.data(),
   483	                       panel_displacements.data(),
   484	                       MPI_DOUBLE,
   485	                       column_communicator);
   486	
   487	        for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
   488	            if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
   489	                continue;
   490	            }
   491	
   492	            const size_t rows = matrix.tileRows(tile_row);
   493	            const double* left_panel = panel.data() + panel_offsets[tile_row];
   494	            for (int tile_col = k + 1; tile_col <= tile_row; ++tile_col) {
   495	                if (tile_col % matrix.process_cols != matrix.my_col) {
   496	                    continue;
   497	                }
   498	
   499	                rankKUpdate(matrix.tile(tile_row, tile_col),
   500	                            left_panel,
   560	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   561	    printf("Options:\n");
   562	    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
   563	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   564	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   565	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   566	}

   568	bool parseSize(const char* text, size_t& value) {
   569	    char* end = nullptr;
   570	    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
   571	    if (end == text || *end != '\0' ||
   572	        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(
   573	                     std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
   574	        return false;
   575	    }
   576	    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
   577	    return true;
   578	}

   580	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   581	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   582	
   583	    int world_rank = 0;
   584	    int world_size = 0;
   585	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &world_rank);
   586	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &world_size);
   587	
   588	    size_t n = 512;
   589	    bool validate = false;
   590	    bool printResults = false;
   591	    bool parse_ok = true;
   592	
   593	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   594	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   595	            parse_ok = parseSize(argv[++i], n);
   596	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   597	            validate = true;
   598	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   599	            printResults = true;
   600	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   601	            if (world_rank == 0) {
   602	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   603	            }
   604	            MPI_Finalize();
   605	            return 0;
   606	        } else {
   607	            parse_ok = false;
   608	        }
   609	        if (!parse_ok) {
   610	            break;
   611	        }
   612	    }
   613	
   614	    if (!parse_ok) {
   615	        if (world_rank == 0) {
   616	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   617	        }
   618	        MPI_Finalize();
   619	        return 1;
   620	    }
   621	
   622	    int dimensions[2] = {0, 0};
   623	    MPI_Dims_create(world_size, 2, dimensions);
   624	    const int periods[2] = {0, 0};
   625	    MPI_Comm cartesian = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   626	    MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dimensions, periods, 0, &cartesian);
   627	
   628	    int rank = 0;
   629	    MPI_Comm_rank(cartesian, &rank);
   630	    const int root_coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
   631	    int root = 0;
   632	    MPI_Cart_rank(cartesian, root_coordinates, &root);
   633	
   634	    int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
   635	    MPI_Cart_coords(cartesian, rank, 2, coordinates);
   636	    MPI_Comm row_communicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   637	    MPI_Comm column_communicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   638	    MPI_Comm_split(cartesian, coordinates[0], coordinates[1], &row_communicator);
   639	    MPI_Comm_split(cartesian, coordinates[1], coordinates[0], &column_communicator);
   640	
   641	    if (rank == root) {
   642	        printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
   643	        printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
   644	        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   645	        printf("MPI processes: %d (%d x %d process grid)\n",
   646	               world_size,
   647	               dimensions[0],
   648	               dimensions[1]);
   649	        printf("Tile size: %d\n", kTileSize);
   650	        printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
   651	        fflush(stdout);
   652	    }
   653	
   654	    std::vector<double> global_matrix;
   655	    std::vector<double> original_matrix;
   656	    if (rank == root) {
   657	        global_matrix.resize(n * n);
   658	        generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(global_matrix, n);
   659	        if (validate) {
   660	            original_matrix = global_matrix;
   661	        }
   662	    }
   663	
   664	    DistributedMatrix local_matrix(n,
   665	                                   kTileSize,
   666	                                   dimensions[0],
   667	                                   dimensions[1],
   668	                                   coordinates[0],
   669	                                   coordinates[1]);
   670	    distributeInitialMatrix(global_matrix, local_matrix, cartesian, rank, root);
   671	    if (rank == root) {
   672	        // The factorization and optional result gather use local_matrix.  Do
   673	        // not keep a second full working copy resident on the input root.
   674	        std::vector<double>().swap(global_matrix);
   675	    }
   676	
   677	    if (rank == root) {
   678	        printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
   679	        fflush(stdout);
   680	    }
   681	    MPI_Barrier(cartesian);
   682	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   683	    const bool success = choleskyDecomposition(local_matrix,
   684	                                               cartesian,
   685	                                               row_communicator,
   686	                                               column_communicator,
   687	                                               rank);
   688	    const double end = MPI_Wtime();
   689	    const double local_elapsed = end - start;
   690	    double elapsed = 0.0;
   691	    MPI_Reduce(&local_elapsed,
   692	               &elapsed,
   693	               1,
   694	               MPI_DOUBLE,
   695	               MPI_MAX,
   696	               root,
   697	               cartesian);
   698	
   699	    int all_success = success ? 1 : 0;
   700	    MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &all_success, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, cartesian);
   701	    if (all_success == 0) {
   702	        if (rank == root) {
   703	            printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
   704	        }
   705	        MPI_Comm_free(&row_communicator);
   706	        MPI_Comm_free(&column_communicator);
   707	        MPI_Comm_free(&cartesian);
   708	        MPI_Finalize();
   709	        return 1;
   710	    }
   711	
   712	    if (rank == root) {
   713	        const long duration_ms = std::max(
   714	            1L, static_cast<long>(std::llround(elapsed * 1000.0)));
   715	        printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration_ms);
   716	
   717	        const double ops = static_cast<double>(n) * n * n / 3.0;
   718	        const double gflops = ops / std::max(elapsed, 1.0e-9) / 1.0e9;
   719	        printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   720	    }
   721	
   722	    std::vector<double> result;
   723	    if (printResults || validate) {
   724	        if (rank == root) {
   725	            result.resize(n * n);
   726	        }
   727	        gatherResult(local_matrix, result, cartesian, rank, root);
   728	
   729	        if (rank == root && printResults) {
   730	            print_results(result, "CholeskyL");
   731	        }
   732	    }
   733	
   734	    int validation_ok = 1;
   735	    if (validate) {
   736	        if (rank == root) {
   737	            printf("Validating result...\n");
   738	            validation_ok = validateCholesky(result, original_matrix, n) ? 1 : 0;
   739	            printf("Validation: %s\n",
   740	                   validation_ok == 1 ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
   741	        }
   742	        MPI_Bcast(&validation_ok, 1, MPI_INT, root, cartesian);
   743	    }
   744	
   745	    MPI_Comm_free(&row_communicator);
   746	    MPI_Comm_free(&column_communicator);
   747	    MPI_Comm_free(&cartesian);
   748	    MPI_Finalize();
   749	    return validation_ok == 1 ? 0 : 1;
   750	}

+struct DistributedMatrix {
+    size_t n = 0;
+    int tile_size = 0;
+    int tile_count = 0;
+    int process_rows = 0;
+    int process_cols = 0;
+    int my_row = 0;
+    int my_col = 0;
+    size_t local_tile_rows = 0;
+    size_t local_tile_cols = 0;
+    size_t tile_elements = 0;
+    std::vector<double> values;
+
+    DistributedMatrix() = default;
+
+    DistributedMatrix(const size_t matrix_size,
+                      const int block_size,
+                      const int grid_rows,
+                      const int grid_cols,
+                      const int process_row,
+                      const int process_col)
+        : n(matrix_size),
+          tile_size(block_size),
+          process_rows(grid_rows),
+          process_cols(grid_cols),
+          my_row(process_row),
+          my_col(process_col),
+          tile_elements(static_cast<size_t>(block_size) * block_size) {
+        const size_t tile_count_size = (n + tile_size - 1) / tile_size;
+        tile_count = static_cast<int>(tile_count_size);
+        local_tile_rows = tile_count <= my_row
+                              ? 0
+                              : 1 + static_cast<size_t>(tile_count - 1 - my_row) /
+                                    process_rows;
+        local_tile_cols = tile_count <= my_col
+                              ? 0
+                              : 1 + static_cast<size_t>(tile_count - 1 - my_col) /
+                                    process_cols;
+        values.resize(local_tile_rows * local_tile_cols * tile_elements, 0.0);
+    }
+
+    size_t tileRows(const int tile_row) const {
+        const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(tile_row) * tile_size;
+        return std::min(static_cast<size_t>(tile_size), n - first);
+    }
+
+    size_t tileCols(const int tile_col) const {
+        const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(tile_col) * tile_size;
+        return std::min(static_cast<size_t>(tile_size), n - first);
+    }
+
+    double* tile(const int tile_row, const int tile_col) {
+        const size_t local_row =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_row - my_row) / process_rows;
+        const size_t local_col =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_col - my_col) / process_cols;
+        const size_t offset =
+            (local_row * local_tile_cols + local_col) * tile_elements;
+        return values.data() + offset;
+    }
+
+    const double* tile(const int tile_row, const int tile_col) const {
+        const size_t local_row =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_row - my_row) / process_rows;
+        const size_t local_col =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_col - my_col) / process_cols;
+        const size_t offset =
+            (local_row * local_tile_cols + local_col) * tile_elements;
+        return values.data() + offset;
+    }
+};
+
+int mpiCount(const size_t count) {
+    if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI message is too large for the MPI count interface\n");
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+    }
+    return static_cast<int>(count);
+}
+
+void packFromGlobal(const std::vector<double>& global,
+                    DistributedMatrix& local) {
+    std::fill(local.values.begin(), local.values.end(), 0.0);
+
+    for (int tile_row = local.my_row; tile_row < local.tile_count;
+         tile_row += local.process_rows) {
+        const size_t rows = local.tileRows(tile_row);
+        for (int tile_col = local.my_col; tile_col < local.tile_count;
+             tile_col += local.process_cols) {
+            if (tile_row < tile_col) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t cols = local.tileCols(tile_col);
+            double* destination = local.tile(tile_row, tile_col);
+            for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+                const size_t global_row =
+                    static_cast<size_t>(tile_row) * local.tile_size + row;
+                for (size_t col = 0; col < cols; ++col) {
+                    const size_t global_col =
+                        static_cast<size_t>(tile_col) * local.tile_size + col;
+                    destination[row * local.tile_size + col] =
+                        global[global_row * local.n + global_col];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void unpackToGlobal(const DistributedMatrix& local,
+                    std::vector<double>& global) {
+    for (int tile_row = local.my_row; tile_row < local.tile_count;
+         tile_row += local.process_rows) {
+        const size_t rows = local.tileRows(tile_row);
+        for (int tile_col = local.my_col; tile_col < local.tile_count;
+             tile_col += local.process_cols) {
+            if (tile_row < tile_col) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t cols = local.tileCols(tile_col);
+            const double* source = local.tile(tile_row, tile_col);
+            for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+                const size_t global_row =
+                    static_cast<size_t>(tile_row) * local.tile_size + row;
+                for (size_t col = 0; col < cols; ++col) {
+                    const size_t global_col =
+                        static_cast<size_t>(tile_col) * local.tile_size + col;
+                    global[global_row * local.n + global_col] =
+                        source[row * local.tile_size + col];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void distributeInitialMatrix(const std::vector<double>& global,
+                             DistributedMatrix& local,
+                             MPI_Comm cartesian,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int root) {
+    const int count = mpiCount(local.values.size());
+
+    if (rank == root) {
+        for (int destination_rank = 0; destination_rank < local.process_rows * local.process_cols;
+             ++destination_rank) {
+            int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+            MPI_Cart_coords(cartesian, destination_rank, 2, coordinates);
+            DistributedMatrix destination(local.n,
+                                          local.tile_size,
+                                          local.process_rows,
+                                          local.process_cols,
+                                          coordinates[0],
+                                          coordinates[1]);
+            packFromGlobal(global, destination);
+
+            const int destination_count = mpiCount(destination.values.size());
+            if (destination_rank == root) {
+                local.values.swap(destination.values);
+            } else {
+                MPI_Send(destination.values.data(),
+                         destination_count,
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         destination_rank,
+                         100,
+                         cartesian);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        MPI_Recv(local.values.data(),
+                 count,
+                 MPI_DOUBLE,
+                 root,
+                 100,
+                 cartesian,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Barrier(cartesian);
+}
+
+void factorDiagonal(double* diagonal,
+                    const size_t block_size,
+                    const int leading_dimension,
+                    bool& success) {
+    success = true;
+
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < block_size; ++row) {
+        for (size_t col = 0; col <= row; ++col) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < col; ++k) {
+                sum += diagonal[row * leading_dimension + k] *
+                       diagonal[col * leading_dimension + k];
+            }
+
+            if (row == col) {
+                const double value =
+                    diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] - sum;
+                if (value <= 0.0) {
+                    success = false;
+                    return;
+                }
+                diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] = sqrt(value);
+            } else {
+                diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] =
+                    (diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] - sum) /
+                    diagonal[col * leading_dimension + col];
+            }
+        }
+
+        for (size_t col = row + 1; col < static_cast<size_t>(leading_dimension);
+             ++col) {
+            diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] = 0.0;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Solve X * L^T = B for each row of B.  L is lower triangular and both B and
+// L are stored row-major with a fixed tile leading dimension.
+void triangularSolve(double* panel_tile,
+                     const double* diagonal,
+                     const size_t rows,
+                     const size_t columns,
+                     const int leading_dimension) {
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        for (size_t col = 0; col < columns; ++col) {
+            double value = panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + col];
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < col; ++k) {
+                value -= panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + k] *
+                         diagonal[col * leading_dimension + k];
+            }
+            panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + col] =
+                value / diagonal[col * leading_dimension + col];
+        }
+        for (size_t col = columns; col < static_cast<size_t>(leading_dimension);
+             ++col) {
+            panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + col] = 0.0;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// C -= A * B^T.  A and B are transposed panel tiles, so the innermost loop
+// operates on contiguous data in both the update tile and B.  This gives the
+// compiler a simple SIMD-friendly kernel without requiring a BLAS dependency.
+void rankKUpdate(double* C,
+                 const double* A_transposed,
+                 const double* B_transposed,
+                 const size_t rows,
+                 const size_t columns,
+                 const size_t inner,
+                 const int leading_dimension) {
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < inner; ++k) {
+        const double* a = A_transposed + k * leading_dimension;
+        const double* b = B_transposed + k * leading_dimension;
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+            double* c = C + row * leading_dimension;
+            const double scalar = a[row];
+#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
+#pragma GCC ivdep
+#endif
+            for (size_t col = 0; col < columns; ++col) {
+                c[col] -= scalar * b[col];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(DistributedMatrix& matrix,
+                           MPI_Comm cartesian,
+                           MPI_Comm row_communicator,
+                           MPI_Comm column_communicator,
+                           const int rank) {
+    const int tile_count = matrix.tile_count;
+    const int leading_dimension = matrix.tile_size;
+    const size_t tile_elements = matrix.tile_elements;
+
+    std::vector<double> diagonal(tile_elements, 0.0);
+    const size_t max_panel_tiles =
+        tile_count == 0
+            ? 0
+            : (static_cast<size_t>(tile_count) + matrix.process_rows - 1) /
+                  matrix.process_rows;
+    std::vector<double> row_panel(std::max<size_t>(1, max_panel_tiles * tile_elements),
+                                  0.0);
+    std::vector<double> panel(std::max<size_t>(1,
+                                               static_cast<size_t>(tile_count) *
+                                                   tile_elements),
+                              0.0);
+    std::vector<int> panel_counts(matrix.process_rows, 0);
+    std::vector<int> panel_displacements(matrix.process_rows, 0);
+    std::vector<int> panel_offsets(tile_count, 0);
+
+    for (int k = 0; k < tile_count; ++k) {
+        const int diagonal_coordinates[2] = {
+            k % matrix.process_rows, k % matrix.process_cols};
+        int diagonal_owner = 0;
+        MPI_Cart_rank(cartesian, diagonal_coordinates, &diagonal_owner);
+
+        int factor_ok = 1;
+        if (rank == diagonal_owner) {
+            const size_t block_size = matrix.tileRows(k);
+            bool local_ok = true;
+            factorDiagonal(matrix.tile(k, k),
+                           block_size,
+                           leading_dimension,
+                           local_ok);
+            factor_ok = local_ok ? 1 : 0;
+            if (!local_ok) {
+                printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+                       static_cast<size_t>(k) * matrix.tile_size);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Bcast(&factor_ok, 1, MPI_INT, diagonal_owner, cartesian);
+        if (factor_ok == 0) {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        if (rank == diagonal_owner) {
+            std::copy(matrix.tile(k, k),
+                      matrix.tile(k, k) + tile_elements,
+                      diagonal.begin());
+        }
+
+        // Every owner of a tile in block column k needs L(k,k).  Only the
+        // process column containing the diagonal participates in this
+        // broadcast.
+        if (matrix.my_col == k % matrix.process_cols) {
+            MPI_Bcast(diagonal.data(),
+                      mpiCount(tile_elements),
+                      MPI_DOUBLE,
+                      k % matrix.process_rows,
+                      column_communicator);
+
+            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                triangularSolve(matrix.tile(tile_row, k),
+                                diagonal.data(),
+                                matrix.tileRows(tile_row),
+                                matrix.tileCols(k),
+                                leading_dimension);
+            }
+        }
+
+        int total_panel_doubles = 0;
+        for (int process_row = 0; process_row < matrix.process_rows;
+             ++process_row) {
+            panel_displacements[process_row] = total_panel_doubles;
+            int row_tile_count = 0;
+            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows == process_row) {
+                    panel_offsets[tile_row] =
+                        total_panel_doubles + row_tile_count *
+                                                     mpiCount(tile_elements);
+                    ++row_tile_count;
+                }
+            }
+            panel_counts[process_row] = row_tile_count * mpiCount(tile_elements);
+            total_panel_doubles += panel_counts[process_row];
+        }
+
+        std::fill(row_panel.begin(), row_panel.end(), 0.0);
+        if (matrix.my_col == k % matrix.process_cols) {
+            int row_tile_number = 0;
+            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                const size_t rows = matrix.tileRows(tile_row);
+                const size_t columns = matrix.tileCols(k);
+                const double* source = matrix.tile(tile_row, k);
+                double* destination =
+                    row_panel.data() + row_tile_number * tile_elements;
+                for (size_t column = 0; column < columns; ++column) {
+                    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+                        destination[column * leading_dimension + row] =
+                            source[row * leading_dimension + column];
+                    }
+                }
+                ++row_tile_number;
+            }
+        }
+
+        // First make each process row's part of the panel available to every
+        // process column, then allgather those parts down each process column.
+        // After these two collectives every process owns a read-only copy of
+        // the current panel, while the factor itself remains distributed.
+        MPI_Bcast(row_panel.data(),
+                  panel_counts[matrix.my_row],
+                  MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  k % matrix.process_cols,
+                  row_communicator);
+        MPI_Allgatherv(row_panel.data(),
+                       panel_counts[matrix.my_row],
+                       MPI_DOUBLE,
+                       panel.data(),
+                       panel_counts.data(),
+                       panel_displacements.data(),
+                       MPI_DOUBLE,
+                       column_communicator);
+
+        for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+            if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t rows = matrix.tileRows(tile_row);
+            const double* left_panel = panel.data() + panel_offsets[tile_row];
+            for (int tile_col = k + 1; tile_col <= tile_row; ++tile_col) {
+                if (tile_col % matrix.process_cols != matrix.my_col) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                rankKUpdate(matrix.tile(tile_row, tile_col),
+                            left_panel,
+                            panel.data() + panel_offsets[tile_col],
+                            rows,
+                            matrix.tileCols(tile_col),
+                            matrix.tileCols(k),
+                            leading_dimension);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
     return true;
 }
 
+void gatherResult(const DistributedMatrix& local,
+                  std::vector<double>& global,
+                  MPI_Comm cartesian,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int root) {
+    const int process_count = local.process_rows * local.process_cols;
+    const int local_count = mpiCount(local.values.size());
+
+    if (rank == root) {
+        std::fill(global.begin(), global.end(), 0.0);
+        unpackToGlobal(local, global);
+
+        for (int source_rank = 0; source_rank < process_count; ++source_rank) {
+            if (source_rank == root) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+            MPI_Cart_coords(cartesian, source_rank, 2, coordinates);
+            DistributedMatrix source(local.n,
+                                     local.tile_size,
+                                     local.process_rows,
+                                     local.process_cols,
+                                     coordinates[0],
+                                     coordinates[1]);
+            MPI_Recv(source.values.data(),
+                     mpiCount(source.values.size()),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     source_rank,
+                     200,
+                     cartesian,
+                     MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            unpackToGlobal(source, global);
+        }
+    } else {
+        MPI_Send(local.values.data(),
+                 local_count,
+                 MPI_DOUBLE,
+                 root,
+                 200,
+                 cartesian);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Barrier(cartesian);
+}

+bool parseSize(const char* text, size_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(
+                     std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

+struct DistributedMatrix {
+    size_t n = 0;
+    int tile_size = 0;
+    int tile_count = 0;
+    int process_rows = 0;
+    int process_cols = 0;
+    int my_row = 0;
+    int my_col = 0;
+    size_t local_tile_rows = 0;
+    size_t local_tile_cols = 0;
+    size_t tile_elements = 0;
+    std::vector<double> values;
+
+    DistributedMatrix() = default;
+
+    DistributedMatrix(const size_t matrix_size,
+                      const int block_size,
+                      const int grid_rows,
+                      const int grid_cols,
+                      const int process_row,
+                      const int process_col)
+        : n(matrix_size),
+          tile_size(block_size),
+          process_rows(grid_rows),
+          process_cols(grid_cols),
+          my_row(process_row),
+          my_col(process_col),
+          tile_elements(static_cast<size_t>(block_size) * block_size) {
+        const size_t tile_count_size = (n + tile_size - 1) / tile_size;
+        tile_count = static_cast<int>(tile_count_size);
+        local_tile_rows = tile_count <= my_row
+                              ? 0
+                              : 1 + static_cast<size_t>(tile_count - 1 - my_row) /
+                                    process_rows;
+        local_tile_cols = tile_count <= my_col
+                              ? 0
+                              : 1 + static_cast<size_t>(tile_count - 1 - my_col) /
+                                    process_cols;
+        values.resize(local_tile_rows * local_tile_cols * tile_elements, 0.0);
+    }
+
+    size_t tileRows(const int tile_row) const {
+        const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(tile_row) * tile_size;
+        return std::min(static_cast<size_t>(tile_size), n - first);
+    }
+
+    size_t tileCols(const int tile_col) const {
+        const size_t first = static_cast<size_t>(tile_col) * tile_size;
+        return std::min(static_cast<size_t>(tile_size), n - first);
+    }
+
+    double* tile(const int tile_row, const int tile_col) {
+        const size_t local_row =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_row - my_row) / process_rows;
+        const size_t local_col =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_col - my_col) / process_cols;
+        const size_t offset =
+            (local_row * local_tile_cols + local_col) * tile_elements;
+        return values.data() + offset;
+    }
+
+    const double* tile(const int tile_row, const int tile_col) const {
+        const size_t local_row =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_row - my_row) / process_rows;
+        const size_t local_col =
+            static_cast<size_t>(tile_col - my_col) / process_cols;
+        const size_t offset =
+            (local_row * local_tile_cols + local_col) * tile_elements;
+        return values.data() + offset;
+    }
+};
+
+int mpiCount(const size_t count) {
+    if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI message is too large for the MPI count interface\n");
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+    }
+    return static_cast<int>(count);
+}
+
+void packFromGlobal(const std::vector<double>& global,
+                    DistributedMatrix& local) {
+    std::fill(local.values.begin(), local.values.end(), 0.0);
+
+    for (int tile_row = local.my_row; tile_row < local.tile_count;
+         tile_row += local.process_rows) {
+        const size_t rows = local.tileRows(tile_row);
+        for (int tile_col = local.my_col; tile_col < local.tile_count;
+             tile_col += local.process_cols) {
+            if (tile_row < tile_col) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t cols = local.tileCols(tile_col);
+            double* destination = local.tile(tile_row, tile_col);
+            for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+                const size_t global_row =
+                    static_cast<size_t>(tile_row) * local.tile_size + row;
+                for (size_t col = 0; col < cols; ++col) {
+                    const size_t global_col =
+                        static_cast<size_t>(tile_col) * local.tile_size + col;
+                    destination[row * local.tile_size + col] =
+                        global[global_row * local.n + global_col];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void unpackToGlobal(const DistributedMatrix& local,
+                    std::vector<double>& global) {
+    for (int tile_row = local.my_row; tile_row < local.tile_count;
+         tile_row += local.process_rows) {
+        const size_t rows = local.tileRows(tile_row);
+        for (int tile_col = local.my_col; tile_col < local.tile_count;
+             tile_col += local.process_cols) {
+            if (tile_row < tile_col) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t cols = local.tileCols(tile_col);
+            const double* source = local.tile(tile_row, tile_col);
+            for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+                const size_t global_row =
+                    static_cast<size_t>(tile_row) * local.tile_size + row;
+                for (size_t col = 0; col < cols; ++col) {
+                    const size_t global_col =
+                        static_cast<size_t>(tile_col) * local.tile_size + col;
+                    global[global_row * local.n + global_col] =
+                        source[row * local.tile_size + col];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void distributeInitialMatrix(const std::vector<double>& global,
+                             DistributedMatrix& local,
+                             MPI_Comm cartesian,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int root) {
+    const int count = mpiCount(local.values.size());
+
+    if (rank == root) {
+        for (int destination_rank = 0; destination_rank < local.process_rows * local.process_cols;
+             ++destination_rank) {
+            int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+            MPI_Cart_coords(cartesian, destination_rank, 2, coordinates);
+            DistributedMatrix destination(local.n,
+                                          local.tile_size,
+                                          local.process_rows,
+                                          local.process_cols,
+                                          coordinates[0],
+                                          coordinates[1]);
+            packFromGlobal(global, destination);
+
+            const int destination_count = mpiCount(destination.values.size());
+            if (destination_rank == root) {
+                local.values.swap(destination.values);
+            } else {
+                MPI_Send(destination.values.data(),
+                         destination_count,
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         destination_rank,
+                         100,
+                         cartesian);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        MPI_Recv(local.values.data(),
+                 count,
+                 MPI_DOUBLE,
+                 root,
+                 100,
+                 cartesian,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Barrier(cartesian);
+}
+
+void factorDiagonal(double* diagonal,
+                    const size_t block_size,
+                    const int leading_dimension,
+                    bool& success) {
+    success = true;
+
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < block_size; ++row) {
+        for (size_t col = 0; col <= row; ++col) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < col; ++k) {
+                sum += diagonal[row * leading_dimension + k] *
+                       diagonal[col * leading_dimension + k];
+            }
+
+            if (row == col) {
+                const double value =
+                    diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] - sum;
+                if (value <= 0.0) {
+                    success = false;
+                    return;
+                }
+                diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] = sqrt(value);
+            } else {
+                diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] =
+                    (diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] - sum) /
+                    diagonal[col * leading_dimension + col];
+            }
+        }
+
+        for (size_t col = row + 1; col < static_cast<size_t>(leading_dimension);
+             ++col) {
+            diagonal[row * leading_dimension + col] = 0.0;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Solve X * L^T = B for each row of B.  L is lower triangular and both B and
+// L are stored row-major with a fixed tile leading dimension.
+void triangularSolve(double* panel_tile,
+                     const double* diagonal,
+                     const size_t rows,
+                     const size_t columns,
+                     const int leading_dimension) {
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        for (size_t col = 0; col < columns; ++col) {
+            double value = panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + col];
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < col; ++k) {
+                value -= panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + k] *
+                         diagonal[col * leading_dimension + k];
+            }
+            panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + col] =
+                value / diagonal[col * leading_dimension + col];
+        }
+        for (size_t col = columns; col < static_cast<size_t>(leading_dimension);
+             ++col) {
+            panel_tile[row * leading_dimension + col] = 0.0;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// C -= A * B^T.  A and B are transposed panel tiles, so the innermost loop
+// operates on contiguous data in both the update tile and B.  This gives the
+// compiler a simple SIMD-friendly kernel without requiring a BLAS dependency.
+void rankKUpdate(double* C,
+                 const double* A_transposed,
+                 const double* B_transposed,
+                 const size_t rows,
+                 const size_t columns,
+                 const size_t inner,
+                 const int leading_dimension) {
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < inner; ++k) {
+        const double* a = A_transposed + k * leading_dimension;
+        const double* b = B_transposed + k * leading_dimension;
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+            double* c = C + row * leading_dimension;
+            const double scalar = a[row];
+#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
+#pragma GCC ivdep
+#endif
+            for (size_t col = 0; col < columns; ++col) {
+                c[col] -= scalar * b[col];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(DistributedMatrix& matrix,
+                           MPI_Comm cartesian,
+                           MPI_Comm row_communicator,
+                           MPI_Comm column_communicator,
+                           const int rank) {
+    const int tile_count = matrix.tile_count;
+    const int leading_dimension = matrix.tile_size;
+    const size_t tile_elements = matrix.tile_elements;
+
+    std::vector<double> diagonal(tile_elements, 0.0);
+    const size_t max_panel_tiles =
+        tile_count == 0
+            ? 0
+            : (static_cast<size_t>(tile_count) + matrix.process_rows - 1) /
+                  matrix.process_rows;
+    std::vector<double> row_panel(std::max<size_t>(1, max_panel_tiles * tile_elements),
+                                  0.0);
+    std::vector<double> panel(std::max<size_t>(1,
+                                               static_cast<size_t>(tile_count) *
+                                                   tile_elements),
+                              0.0);
+    std::vector<int> panel_counts(matrix.process_rows, 0);
+    std::vector<int> panel_displacements(matrix.process_rows, 0);
+    std::vector<int> panel_offsets(tile_count, 0);
+
+    for (int k = 0; k < tile_count; ++k) {
+        const int diagonal_coordinates[2] = {
+            k % matrix.process_rows, k % matrix.process_cols};
+        int diagonal_owner = 0;
+        MPI_Cart_rank(cartesian, diagonal_coordinates, &diagonal_owner);
+
+        int factor_ok = 1;
+        if (rank == diagonal_owner) {
+            const size_t block_size = matrix.tileRows(k);
+            bool local_ok = true;
+            factorDiagonal(matrix.tile(k, k),
+                           block_size,
+                           leading_dimension,
+                           local_ok);
+            factor_ok = local_ok ? 1 : 0;
+            if (!local_ok) {
+                printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+                       static_cast<size_t>(k) * matrix.tile_size);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Bcast(&factor_ok, 1, MPI_INT, diagonal_owner, cartesian);
+        if (factor_ok == 0) {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        if (rank == diagonal_owner) {
+            std::copy(matrix.tile(k, k),
+                      matrix.tile(k, k) + tile_elements,
+                      diagonal.begin());
+        }
+
+        // Every owner of a tile in block column k needs L(k,k).  Only the
+        // process column containing the diagonal participates in this
+        // broadcast.
+        if (matrix.my_col == k % matrix.process_cols) {
+            MPI_Bcast(diagonal.data(),
+                      mpiCount(tile_elements),
+                      MPI_DOUBLE,
+                      k % matrix.process_rows,
+                      column_communicator);
+
+            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                triangularSolve(matrix.tile(tile_row, k),
+                                diagonal.data(),
+                                matrix.tileRows(tile_row),
+                                matrix.tileCols(k),
+                                leading_dimension);
+            }
+        }
+
+        int total_panel_doubles = 0;
+        for (int process_row = 0; process_row < matrix.process_rows;
+             ++process_row) {
+            panel_displacements[process_row] = total_panel_doubles;
+            int row_tile_count = 0;
+            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows == process_row) {
+                    panel_offsets[tile_row] =
+                        total_panel_doubles + row_tile_count *
+                                                     mpiCount(tile_elements);
+                    ++row_tile_count;
+                }
+            }
+            panel_counts[process_row] = row_tile_count * mpiCount(tile_elements);
+            total_panel_doubles += panel_counts[process_row];
+        }
+
+        std::fill(row_panel.begin(), row_panel.end(), 0.0);
+        if (matrix.my_col == k % matrix.process_cols) {
+            int row_tile_number = 0;
+            for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+                if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                const size_t rows = matrix.tileRows(tile_row);
+                const size_t columns = matrix.tileCols(k);
+                const double* source = matrix.tile(tile_row, k);
+                double* destination =
+                    row_panel.data() + row_tile_number * tile_elements;
+                for (size_t column = 0; column < columns; ++column) {
+                    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+                        destination[column * leading_dimension + row] =
+                            source[row * leading_dimension + column];
+                    }
+                }
+                ++row_tile_number;
+            }
+        }
+
+        // First make each process row's part of the panel available to every
+        // process column, then allgather those parts down each process column.
+        // After these two collectives every process owns a read-only copy of
+        // the current panel, while the factor itself remains distributed.
+        MPI_Bcast(row_panel.data(),
+                  panel_counts[matrix.my_row],
+                  MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  k % matrix.process_cols,
+                  row_communicator);
+        MPI_Allgatherv(row_panel.data(),
+                       panel_counts[matrix.my_row],
+                       MPI_DOUBLE,
+                       panel.data(),
+                       panel_counts.data(),
+                       panel_displacements.data(),
+                       MPI_DOUBLE,
+                       column_communicator);
+
+        for (int tile_row = k + 1; tile_row < tile_count; ++tile_row) {
+            if (tile_row % matrix.process_rows != matrix.my_row) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const size_t rows = matrix.tileRows(tile_row);
+            const double* left_panel = panel.data() + panel_offsets[tile_row];
+            for (int tile_col = k + 1; tile_col <= tile_row; ++tile_col) {
+                if (tile_col % matrix.process_cols != matrix.my_col) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                rankKUpdate(matrix.tile(tile_row, tile_col),
+                            left_panel,
+                            panel.data() + panel_offsets[tile_col],
+                            rows,
+                            matrix.tileCols(tile_col),
+                            matrix.tileCols(k),
+                            leading_dimension);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    return true;
+}
+
+void gatherResult(const DistributedMatrix& local,
+                  std::vector<double>& global,
+                  MPI_Comm cartesian,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int root) {
+    const int process_count = local.process_rows * local.process_cols;
+    const int local_count = mpiCount(local.values.size());
+
+    if (rank == root) {
+        std::fill(global.begin(), global.end(), 0.0);
+        unpackToGlobal(local, global);
+
+        for (int source_rank = 0; source_rank < process_count; ++source_rank) {
+            if (source_rank == root) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            int coordinates[2] = {0, 0};
+            MPI_Cart_coords(cartesian, source_rank, 2, coordinates);
+            DistributedMatrix source(local.n,
+                                     local.tile_size,
+                                     local.process_rows,
+                                     local.process_cols,
+                                     coordinates[0],
+                                     coordinates[1]);
+            MPI_Recv(source.values.data(),
+                     mpiCount(source.values.size()),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     source_rank,
+                     200,
+                     cartesian,
+                     MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            unpackToGlobal(source, global);
+        }
+    } else {
+        MPI_Send(local.values.data(),
+                 local_count,
+                 MPI_DOUBLE,
+                 root,
+                 200,
+                 cartesian);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Barrier(cartesian);
+}
+

+bool parseSize(const char* text, size_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (end == text || *end != '\0' ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(
+                     std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// A square 2-D block-cyclic distribution gives every process a comparable
+// amount of work and keeps the distributed factor at O(n^2 / P) storage.  A
+// tile is deliberately large enough to make the local rank-k updates compute
